grep -n "marker\|stripe\|#fff\|#1C2735\|caret" base.css | cut -c1-200&lt;/parameter&gt;
&lt;parameter name="description"&gt;Apply PBIPatika palette tokens to base CSS&lt;/parameter&gt;
&lt;/tool&gt;
Şimdi grafik kütüphanesini yazıyorum (sütun, çubuk, çizgi, alan, halka, kombo, şelale, huni, ağaç haritası, gösterge, dağılım, matris, kart, KPI).
&lt;tool name="Write"&gt;
&lt;parameter name="content"&gt;/* PBIPatika — SVG görsel kütüphanesi. Tüm görseller tema değişkenleriyle boyanır. */
(function (root) {
  'use strict';
  const esc = s =&gt; String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/[&amp;&lt;&gt;"]/g, c =&gt; ({ '&amp;': '&amp;amp;', '&lt;': '&amp;lt;', '&gt;': '&amp;gt;', '"': '&amp;quot;' }[c]));
  const S = i =&gt; 'var(--s' + ((i % 8) + 1) + ')';
  const isNum = v =&gt; typeof v === 'number' &amp;&amp; isFinite(v);
  const g3 = s =&gt; s.replace(/\B(?=(\d{3})+(?!\d))/g, '.');
  function nf(v, dec) { const neg = v &lt; 0; let [i, f] = Math.abs(v).toFixed(dec).split('.'); return (neg ? '−' : '') + g3(i) + (f ? ',' + f : ''); }
  function fmt(v, kind, short) {
    if (v === null || v === undefined || (typeof v === 'number' &amp;&amp; isNaN(v))) return '(Boş)';
    if (typeof v !== 'number') { if (v instanceof Date) return root.PBI_DAX ? root.PBI_DAX.fmtDate(v, 'dd.MM.yyyy') : String(v); if (typeof v === 'boolean') return v ? 'TRUE' : 'FALSE'; return String(v); }
    if (!isFinite(v)) return v &gt; 0 ? '∞' : '−∞';
    if (kind === 'yuzde') return nf(v * 100, Math.abs(v) &lt; 0.1 ? 1 : 1) + '%';
    if (kind === 'para') { const a = Math.abs(v); if (short &amp;&amp; a &gt;= 1e6) return nf(v / 1e6, 2) + ' Mn ₺'; if (short &amp;&amp; a &gt;= 1e3) return nf(v / 1e3, 1) + ' B ₺'; return nf(v, a &lt; 100 ? 2 : 0) + ' ₺'; }
    if (kind === 'adet') { if (short &amp;&amp; Math.abs(v) &gt;= 1e4) return nf(v / 1e3, 1) + ' B'; return nf(v, Number.isInteger(v) ? 0 : 1); }
    if (Number.isInteger(v)) return nf(v, 0);
    return nf(v, Math.abs(v) &lt; 1 ? 3 : 2);
  }
  function niceTicks(min, max, n) {
    if (min === max) { max = min + 1; }
    const span = max - min; const step0 = span / (n || 4); const mag = Math.pow(10, Math.floor(Math.log10(step0))); const r = step0 / mag;
    const step = (r &lt;= 1 ? 1 : r &lt;= 2 ? 2 : r &lt;= 2.5 ? 2.5 : r &lt;= 5 ? 5 : 10) * mag;
    const lo = Math.floor(min / step) * step, hi = Math.ceil(max / step) * step; const t = [];
    for (let x = lo; x &lt;= hi + step / 2; x += step) t.push(Math.round(x / step) * step);
    return t;
  }
  const tip = (label, rows) =&gt; ' data-tip="' + esc(JSON.stringify({ h: label, r: rows })) + '"';
  const svgOpen = (W, H, label) =&gt; '&lt;svg class="viz" viewBox="0 0 ' + W + ' ' + H + '" role="img" aria-label="' + esc(label || 'Grafik') + '" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet"&gt;';
  const txt = (x, y, s, cls, anchor, extra) =&gt; '&lt;text x="' + x + '" y="' + y + '" class="' + (cls || 'vt') + '"' + (anchor ? ' text-anchor="' + anchor + '"' : '') + (extra || '') + '&gt;' + esc(s) + '&lt;/text&gt;';
  const trunc = (s, n) =&gt; { s = String(s); return s.length &gt; n ? s.slice(0, n - 1) + '…' : s; };
  function legend(series) { if (series.length &lt; 2) return ''; return '&lt;div class="vlegend"&gt;' + series.map((s, i) =&gt; '&lt;span&gt;&lt;i style="background:' + (s.color || S(i)) + '"&gt;&lt;/i&gt;' + esc(s.name) + '&lt;/span&gt;').join('') + '&lt;/div&gt;'; }
  const roundTopRect = (x, y, w, h, r, fill, extra) =&gt; { if (h &lt;= 0 || w &lt;= 0) return ''; r = Math.min(r, w / 2, h); return '&lt;path d="M' + x + ',' + (y + h) + 'V' + (y + r) + 'Q' + x + ',' + y + ' ' + (x + r) + ',' + y + 'H' + (x + w - r) + 'Q' + (x + w) + ',' + y + ' ' + (x + w) + ',' + (y + r) + 'V' + (y + h) + 'Z" fill="' + fill + '"' + (extra || '') + '/&gt;'; };
  const roundRightRect = (x, y, w, h, r, fill, extra) =&gt; { if (h &lt;= 0 || w &lt;= 0) return ''; r = Math.min(r, h / 2, w); return '&lt;path d="M' + x + ',' + y + 'H' + (x + w - r) + 'Q' + (x + w) + ',' + y + ' ' + (x + w) + ',' + (y + r) + 'V' + (y + h - r) + 'Q' + (x + w) + ',' + (y + h) + ' ' + (x + w - r) + ',' + (y + h) + 'H' + x + 'Z" fill="' + fill + '"' + (extra || '') + '/&gt;'; };
  const selCls = (o, i) =&gt; (o.sel != null ? (o.sel === i ? ' sel' : ' dim') : '');

  /* Sütun (dikey çubuk) — kümelenmiş ya da yığılmış; isteğe bağlı çizgi serileri (kombo) */
  function column(d, o) {
    o = o || {}; const W = o.W || 420, H = o.H || 240, padL = 48, padR = 10, padT = 14, padB = 38;
    const cats = d.cats; const bars = d.series.filter(s =&gt; s.type !== 'line'); const lines = d.series.filter(s =&gt; s.type === 'line');
    const stacked = o.stacked; let vals = [];
    if (stacked) cats.forEach((c, i) =&gt; { let p = 0, n = 0; bars.forEach(s =&gt; { const v = s.vals[i] || 0; if (v &gt; 0) p += v; else n += v; }); vals.push(p, n); });
    else bars.forEach(s =&gt; vals.push(...s.vals.filter(isNum)));
    lines.forEach(s =&gt; vals.push(...s.vals.filter(isNum)));
    let mn = Math.min(0, ...vals), mx = Math.max(0, ...vals); if (mx === mn) mx = mn + 1;
    const ticks = niceTicks(mn, mx, 4); mn = ticks[0]; mx = ticks[ticks.length - 1];
    const iw = W - padL - padR, ih = H - padT - padB; const y = v =&gt; padT + ih - (v - mn) / (mx - mn) * ih;
    const bw = iw / Math.max(1, cats.length); const gw = Math.max(2, bw * (cats.length &gt; 12 ? 0.78 : 0.66)); const sw = stacked ? gw : (gw - 2 * (bars.length - 1)) / Math.max(1, bars.length);
    let h = svgOpen(W, H, o.label);
    ticks.forEach(t =&gt; { h += '&lt;line x1="' + padL + '" x2="' + (W - padR) + '" y1="' + y(t) + '" y2="' + y(t) + '" class="' + (t === 0 ? 'vbase' : 'vgrid') + '"/&gt;' + txt(padL - 6, y(t) + 3.5, fmt(t, d.fmt, true), 'vt vax', 'end'); });
    const every = Math.ceil(cats.length / Math.floor(iw / 34));
    cats.forEach((c, i) =&gt; {
      const x0 = padL + i * bw + (bw - gw) / 2; let pos = 0, neg = 0;
      const rows = d.series.map((s, k) =&gt; [s.name, fmt(s.vals[i], s.fmt || d.fmt)]);
      h += '&lt;g class="vmark' + selCls(o, i) + '" data-i="' + i + '"' + tip(c, rows) + ' tabindex="0"&gt;';
      h += '&lt;rect x="' + (padL + i * bw) + '" y="' + padT + '" width="' + bw + '" height="' + ih + '" fill="transparent"/&gt;';
      bars.forEach((s, k) =&gt; {
        const v = s.vals[i]; if (!isNum(v) || v === 0) return; const col = s.color || S(d.series.indexOf(s));
        if (stacked) { const a = v &gt; 0 ? pos : neg; const b = a + v; if (v &gt; 0) pos = b; else neg = b; const yt = y(Math.max(a, b)), yb = y(Math.min(a, b)); h += '&lt;rect x="' + x0 + '" y="' + yt + '" width="' + gw + '" height="' + Math.max(0, yb - yt - 1) + '" fill="' + col + '"/&gt;'; }
        else { const xx = x0 + k * (sw + 2); if (v &gt;= 0) h += roundTopRect(xx, y(v), sw, y(0) - y(v), 3, col); else h += '&lt;rect x="' + xx + '" y="' + y(0) + '" width="' + sw + '" height="' + (y(v) - y(0)) + '" fill="' + col + '" rx="2"/&gt;'; }
      });
      h += '&lt;/g&gt;';
      if (i % every === 0) h += txt(padL + i * bw + bw / 2, H - padB + 15, trunc(c, cats.length &gt; 8 ? 7 : 12), 'vt vcat', 'middle');
    });
    lines.forEach(s =&gt; { const k = d.series.indexOf(s); const col = s.color || S(k); let path = ''; s.vals.forEach((v, i) =&gt; { if (!isNum(v)) return; path += (path ? 'L' : 'M') + (padL + i * bw + bw / 2) + ',' + y(v); }); h += '&lt;path d="' + path + '" fill="none" stroke="' + col + '" stroke-width="2"' + (s.dash ? ' stroke-dasharray="5 4"' : '') + '/&gt;'; s.vals.forEach((v, i) =&gt; { if (isNum(v) &amp;&amp; cats.length &lt;= 16) h += '&lt;circle cx="' + (padL + i * bw + bw / 2) + '" cy="' + y(v) + '" r="3.5" fill="' + col + '" stroke="var(--surface)" stroke-width="1.5" pointer-events="none"/&gt;'; }); });
    if (o.labels &amp;&amp; !stacked &amp;&amp; bars.length === 1 &amp;&amp; cats.length &lt;= 8) bars[0].vals.forEach((v, i) =&gt; { if (isNum(v)) h += txt(padL + i * bw + bw / 2, y(Math.max(0, v)) - 5, fmt(v, d.fmt, true), 'vt vlab', 'middle'); });
    return h + '&lt;/svg&gt;' + legend(d.series);
  }
  /* Çubuk (yatay) */
  function bar(d, o) {
    o = o || {}; const cats = d.cats; const stacked = o.stacked, pct = o.pct; const series = d.series;
    const rowH = o.rowH || (cats.length &gt; 10 ? 20 : 26); const W = o.W || 420, padL = Math.min(150, 14 + 6.2 * Math.max(...cats.map(c =&gt; Math.min(22, String(c).length)))), padR = 56, padT = 6;
    const H = padT + rowH * cats.length + 22; const iw = W - padL - padR;
    let totals = cats.map((c, i) =&gt; series.reduce((a, s) =&gt; a + (isNum(s.vals[i]) ? s.vals[i] : 0), 0));
    let mx = pct ? 1 : stacked ? Math.max(...totals, 0) : Math.max(0, ...series.flatMap(s =&gt; s.vals.filter(isNum)));
    let mn = pct || stacked ? 0 : Math.min(0, ...series.flatMap(s =&gt; s.vals.filter(isNum)));
    if (mx === mn) mx = mn + 1; const ticks = pct ? [0, .25, .5, .75, 1] : niceTicks(mn, mx, 3); mn = ticks[0]; mx = ticks[ticks.length - 1];
    const x = v =&gt; padL + (v - mn) / (mx - mn) * iw;
    let h = svgOpen(W, H, o.label);
    ticks.forEach(t =&gt; { h += '&lt;line x1="' + x(t) + '" x2="' + x(t) + '" y1="' + padT + '" y2="' + (H - 20) + '" class="' + (t === 0 ? 'vbase' : 'vgrid') + '"/&gt;' + txt(x(t), H - 6, pct ? Math.round(t * 100) + '%' : fmt(t, d.fmt, true), 'vt vax', 'middle'); });
    cats.forEach((c, i) =&gt; {
      const y0 = padT + i * rowH, bh = rowH * 0.66, yb = y0 + (rowH - bh) / 2;
      const rows = series.map(s =&gt; [s.name, fmt(s.vals[i], s.fmt || d.fmt)]); if (pct) rows.push(['Toplam', fmt(totals[i], d.fmt)]);
      h += '&lt;g class="vmark' + selCls(o, i) + '" data-i="' + i + '"' + tip(c, rows) + ' tabindex="0"&gt;&lt;rect x="0" y="' + y0 + '" width="' + W + '" height="' + rowH + '" fill="transparent"/&gt;';
      h += txt(padL - 8, y0 + rowH / 2 + 4, trunc(c, 22), 'vt vcat', 'end');
      if (stacked || pct) { let acc = 0; series.forEach((s, k) =&gt; { let v = s.vals[i]; if (!isNum(v) || v &lt;= 0) return; if (pct) v = totals[i] ? v / totals[i] : 0; const x1 = x(acc), x2 = x(acc + v); acc += v; h += '&lt;rect x="' + x1 + '" y="' + yb + '" width="' + Math.max(0, x2 - x1 - 1) + '" height="' + bh + '" fill="' + (s.color || S(k)) + '"/&gt;'; }); if (!pct) h += txt(x(acc) + 5, y0 + rowH / 2 + 4, fmt(totals[i], d.fmt, true), 'vt vlab'); }
      else { const sh = (bh - (series.length - 1) * 2) / series.length; series.forEach((s, k) =&gt; { const v = s.vals[i]; if (!isNum(v)) return; const xs = x(Math.min(0, v)), w = Math.abs(x(v) - x(0)); h += roundRightRect(xs, yb + k * (sh + 2), w, sh, 3, s.color || (o.colorBySign ? (v &lt; 0 ? 'var(--err)' : 'var(--ok)') : S(k))); if (series.length === 1) h += txt(x(Math.max(0, v)) + 5, y0 + rowH / 2 + 4, fmt(v, d.fmt, true), 'vt vlab'); }); }
      h += '&lt;/g&gt;';
    });
    return h + '&lt;/svg&gt;' + legend(series);
  }
  /* Çizgi / alan */
  function line(d, o) {
    o = o || {}; const W = o.W || 420, H = o.H || 230, padL = 50, padR = 14, padT = 14, padB = 34;
    const cats = d.cats; const all = d.series.flatMap(s =&gt; s.vals.filter(isNum)); let mn = Math.min(o.area ? 0 : Infinity, ...all), mx = Math.max(...all, 0);
    if (!isFinite(mn)) mn = 0; if (!o.area &amp;&amp; mn &gt; 0) mn = Math.min(0, mn); if (mx === mn) mx = mn + 1;
    const ticks = niceTicks(mn, mx, 4); mn = ticks[0]; mx = ticks[ticks.length - 1];
    const iw = W - padL - padR, ih = H - padT - padB; const step = cats.length &gt; 1 ? iw / (cats.length - 1) : 0;
    const X = i =&gt; padL + (cats.length &gt; 1 ? i * step : iw / 2), Y = v =&gt; padT + ih - (v - mn) / (mx - mn) * ih;
    let h = svgOpen(W, H, o.label);
    ticks.forEach(t =&gt; { h += '&lt;line x1="' + padL + '" x2="' + (W - padR) + '" y1="' + Y(t) + '" y2="' + Y(t) + '" class="' + (t === 0 ? 'vbase' : 'vgrid') + '"/&gt;' + txt(padL - 6, Y(t) + 3.5, fmt(t, d.fmt, true), 'vt vax', 'end'); });
    const every = Math.ceil(cats.length / Math.floor(iw / 40));
    cats.forEach((c, i) =&gt; { if (i % every === 0 || i === cats.length - 1 &amp;&amp; (cats.length - 1) % every &gt; every / 2) h += txt(X(i), H - padB + 16, trunc(c, 8), 'vt vcat', 'middle'); });
    d.series.forEach((s, k) =&gt; {
      const col = s.color || S(k); let path = '', first = null, last = null;
      s.vals.forEach((v, i) =&gt; { if (!isNum(v)) return; if (first === null) first = i; last = i; path += (path ? 'L' : 'M') + X(i).toFixed(1) + ',' + Y(v).toFixed(1); });
      if (!path) return;
      if (o.area &amp;&amp; d.series.length === 1) h += '&lt;path d="' + path + 'L' + X(last) + ',' + Y(Math.max(mn, 0)) + 'L' + X(first) + ',' + Y(Math.max(mn, 0)) + 'Z" fill="' + col + '" opacity=".14"/&gt;';
      h += '&lt;path d="' + path + '" fill="none" stroke="' + col + '" stroke-width="2" stroke-linejoin="round"' + (s.dash ? ' stroke-dasharray="5 4"' : '') + '/&gt;';
      if (last !== null) h += '&lt;circle cx="' + X(last) + '" cy="' + Y(s.vals[last]) + '" r="4" fill="' + col + '" stroke="var(--surface)" stroke-width="2"/&gt;';
    });
    // hover kolonları
    cats.forEach((c, i) =&gt; { const w = cats.length &gt; 1 ? step : iw; h += '&lt;g class="vmark vcol' + selCls(o, i) + '" data-i="' + i + '"' + tip(c, d.series.map(s =&gt; [s.name, fmt(s.vals[i], s.fmt || d.fmt)])) + '&gt;&lt;rect x="' + (X(i) - w / 2) + '" y="' + padT + '" width="' + w + '" height="' + ih + '" fill="transparent"/&gt;&lt;line x1="' + X(i) + '" x2="' + X(i) + '" y1="' + padT + '" y2="' + (padT + ih) + '" class="vcross"/&gt;&lt;/g&gt;'; });
    return h + '&lt;/svg&gt;' + legend(d.series);
  }
  /* Halka */
  function donut(d, o) {
    o = o || {}; const W = o.W || 420, H = o.H || 220, cx = 110, cy = H / 2, R = Math.min(90, H / 2 - 12), r = R * 0.6;
    const vals = d.series[0].vals.map(v =&gt; isNum(v) &amp;&amp; v &gt; 0 ? v : 0); const tot = vals.reduce((a, b) =&gt; a + b, 0) || 1;
    let h = svgOpen(W, H, o.label); let a0 = -Math.PI / 2;
    const order = vals.map((v, i) =&gt; i);
    order.forEach(i =&gt; {
      const v = vals[i]; if (!v) return; const a1 = a0 + v / tot * Math.PI * 2; const gap = 0.012;
      const p = (rr, a) =&gt; (cx + rr * Math.cos(a)).toFixed(2) + ',' + (cy + rr * Math.sin(a)).toFixed(2);
      const large = a1 - a0 &gt; Math.PI ? 1 : 0; const s0 = a0 + gap, s1 = a1 - gap;
      const path = 'M' + p(R, s0) + 'A' + R + ',' + R + ' 0 ' + large + ' 1 ' + p(R, s1) + 'L' + p(r, s1) + 'A' + r + ',' + r + ' 0 ' + large + ' 0 ' + p(r, s0) + 'Z';
      h += '&lt;path class="vmark' + selCls(o, i) + '" data-i="' + i + '" d="' + path + '" fill="' + S(i) + '"' + tip(d.cats[i], [[d.series[0].name, fmt(d.series[0].vals[i], d.fmt)], ['Pay', fmt(v / tot, 'yuzde')]]) + ' tabindex="0"/&gt;';
      a0 = a1;
    });
    h += txt(cx, cy - 2, fmt(tot, d.fmt, true), 'vt vbig', 'middle') + txt(cx, cy + 15, 'Toplam', 'vt vax', 'middle');
    const lx = 228; const lh = Math.min(22, (H - 20) / Math.max(1, d.cats.length));
    d.cats.forEach((c, i) =&gt; { const yy = 14 + i * lh + lh / 2; h += '&lt;g class="vmark' + selCls(o, i) + '" data-i="' + i + '"' + tip(c, [[d.series[0].name, fmt(d.series[0].vals[i], d.fmt)], ['Pay', fmt(vals[i] / tot, 'yuzde')]]) + '&gt;&lt;rect x="' + lx + '" y="' + (yy - 6) + '" width="10" height="10" rx="2" fill="' + S(i) + '"/&gt;' + txt(lx + 16, yy + 3, trunc(c, 15), 'vt vcat') + txt(W - 6, yy + 3, fmt(vals[i] / tot, 'yuzde'), 'vt vlab', 'end') + '&lt;/g&gt;'; });
    return h + '&lt;/svg&gt;';
  }
  /* Kart ve KPI */
  function card(v, label, kind, sub) { return '&lt;div class="vcard"&gt;&lt;b&gt;' + esc(fmt(v, kind, true)) + '&lt;/b&gt;&lt;span&gt;' + esc(label) + '&lt;/span&gt;' + (sub || '') + '&lt;/div&gt;'; }
  function kpi(v, target, label, kind, tlabel) {
    const ok = isNum(v) &amp;&amp; isNum(target) &amp;&amp; v &gt;= target; const diff = isNum(v) &amp;&amp; isNum(target) &amp;&amp; target ? v / target - 1 : null;
    const state = diff === null ? '' : ok ? 'ok' : diff &gt; -0.05 ? 'warn' : 'bad';
    const icon = state === 'ok' ? '▲' : state === 'warn' ? '■' : state === 'bad' ? '▼' : '';
    return '&lt;div class="vcard kpi ' + state + '"&gt;&lt;b&gt;' + esc(fmt(v, kind, true)) + '&lt;/b&gt;&lt;span&gt;' + esc(label) + '&lt;/span&gt;' + (diff !== null ? '&lt;em class="pill ' + state + '"&gt;' + icon + ' ' + (diff &gt;= 0 ? '+' : '') + fmt(diff, 'yuzde') + ' &lt;small&gt;' + esc(tlabel || 'hedefe göre') + '&lt;/small&gt;&lt;/em&gt;' : '') + '&lt;/div&gt;';
  }
  /* Gösterge (yarım daire) */
  function gauge(v, target, max, kind, label) {
    const W = 260, H = 150, cx = 130, cy = 128, R = 100, r = 72; max = max || Math.max(v || 0, target || 0) * 1.25 || 1;
    const ang = t =&gt; Math.PI + Math.min(1, Math.max(0, t / max)) * Math.PI;
    const p = (rr, a) =&gt; (cx + rr * Math.cos(a)).toFixed(2) + ',' + (cy + rr * Math.sin(a)).toFixed(2);
    const arc = (a0, a1, col) =&gt; 'M' + p(R, a0) + 'A' + R + ',' + R + ' 0 0 1 ' + p(R, a1) + 'L' + p(r, a1) + 'A' + r + ',' + r + ' 0 0 0 ' + p(r, a0) + 'Z';
    let h = svgOpen(W, H, label) + '&lt;path d="' + arc(Math.PI, 2 * Math.PI) + '" fill="var(--grid)"/&gt;';
    if (isNum(v) &amp;&amp; v &gt; 0) h += '&lt;path d="' + arc(Math.PI, ang(v)) + '" fill="var(--s1)"' + tip(label, [['Değer', fmt(v, kind)], ['Hedef', fmt(target, kind)]]) + ' class="vmark"/&gt;';
    if (isNum(target)) { const a = ang(target); h += '&lt;line x1="' + p(r - 6, a).split(',')[0] + '" y1="' + p(r - 6, a).split(',')[1] + '" x2="' + p(R + 6, a).split(',')[0] + '" y2="' + p(R + 6, a).split(',')[1] + '" stroke="var(--ink)" stroke-width="2.5"/&gt;'; }
    h += txt(cx, cy - 18, fmt(v, kind, true), 'vt vbig', 'middle') + txt(cx - R + 14, cy + 16, '0', 'vt vax', 'middle') + txt(cx + R - 14, cy + 16, fmt(max, kind, true), 'vt vax', 'middle');
    if (isNum(target)) h += txt(cx, cy + 4, 'Hedef ' + fmt(target, kind, true), 'vt vax', 'middle');
    return h + '&lt;/svg&gt;';
  }
  /* Şelale */
  function waterfall(d, o) {
    o = o || {}; const cats = d.cats.concat(['Toplam']); const vals = d.series[0].vals.map(v =&gt; isNum(v) ? v : 0);
    const W = o.W || 420, H = o.H || 240, padL = 50, padR = 10, padT = 14, padB = 40; let acc = 0; const segs = vals.map(v =&gt; { const s = { a: acc, b: acc + v, v }; acc += v; return s; }); segs.push({ a: 0, b: acc, v: acc, total: true });
    if (o.start != null) { segs.forEach(s =&gt; { if (!s.total) { s.a += o.start; s.b += o.start; } }); segs.unshift({ a: 0, b: o.start, v: o.start, total: true, label: o.startLabel }); segs[segs.length - 1].b += o.start; segs[segs.length - 1].v += o.start; cats.unshift(o.startLabel || 'Başlangıç'); }
    const all = segs.flatMap(s =&gt; [s.a, s.b]); let mn = Math.min(0, ...all), mx = Math.max(...all); const ticks = niceTicks(mn, mx, 4); mn = ticks[0]; mx = ticks[ticks.length - 1];
    const iw = W - padL - padR, ih = H - padT - padB; const Y = v =&gt; padT + ih - (v - mn) / (mx - mn) * ih; const bw = iw / segs.length;
    let h = svgOpen(W, H, o.label);
    ticks.forEach(t =&gt; { h += '&lt;line x1="' + padL + '" x2="' + (W - padR) + '" y1="' + Y(t) + '" y2="' + Y(t) + '" class="' + (t === 0 ? 'vbase' : 'vgrid') + '"/&gt;' + txt(padL - 6, Y(t) + 3.5, fmt(t, d.fmt, true), 'vt vax', 'end'); });
    segs.forEach((s, i) =&gt; { const x = padL + i * bw + bw * 0.18, w = bw * 0.64; const top = Y(Math.max(s.a, s.b)), bot = Y(Math.min(s.a, s.b)); const col = s.total ? 'var(--s1)' : s.v &gt;= 0 ? 'var(--ok)' : 'var(--err)';
      h += '&lt;g class="vmark" data-i="' + i + '"' + tip(cats[i], [[s.total ? 'Toplam' : 'Değişim', fmt(s.v, d.fmt)]]) + ' tabindex="0"&gt;&lt;rect x="' + x + '" y="' + top + '" width="' + w + '" height="' + Math.max(1, bot - top) + '" fill="' + col + '" rx="2"/&gt;&lt;/g&gt;';
      if (i &lt; segs.length - 1) h += '&lt;line x1="' + (x + w) + '" x2="' + (x + bw) + '" y1="' + Y(s.b) + '" y2="' + Y(s.b) + '" class="vgrid" stroke-dasharray="2 2"/&gt;';
      h += txt(padL + i * bw + bw / 2, H - padB + 15, trunc(cats[i], 8), 'vt vcat', 'middle');
    });
    return h + '&lt;/svg&gt;&lt;div class="vlegend"&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;i style="background:var(--ok)"&gt;&lt;/i&gt;Artış&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;i style="background:var(--err)"&gt;&lt;/i&gt;Azalış&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;i style="background:var(--s1)"&gt;&lt;/i&gt;Toplam&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;';
  }
  /* Huni */
  function funnel(d, o) {
    o = o || {}; const vals = d.series[0].vals.map(v =&gt; isNum(v) ? v : 0); const mx = Math.max(...vals, 1); const W = o.W || 420, rowH = 34, H = rowH * vals.length + 6;
    let h = svgOpen(W, H, o.label);
    vals.forEach((v, i) =&gt; { const w = Math.max(4, v / mx * (W - 150)); const x = 110 + (W - 150 - w) / 2; const y = 3 + i * rowH;
      h += '&lt;g class="vmark' + selCls(o, i) + '" data-i="' + i + '"' + tip(d.cats[i], [[d.series[0].name, fmt(v, d.fmt)], ['İlk adıma oran', fmt(v / (vals[0] || 1), 'yuzde')]]) + ' tabindex="0"&gt;&lt;rect x="' + x + '" y="' + y + '" width="' + w + '" height="' + (rowH - 4) + '" rx="3" fill="var(--s1)" opacity="' + (1 - i * 0.12) + '"/&gt;' + txt(102, y + rowH / 2 + 2, trunc(d.cats[i], 14), 'vt vcat', 'end') + txt(W - 4, y + rowH / 2 + 2, fmt(v, d.fmt, true), 'vt vlab', 'end') + '&lt;/g&gt;'; });
    return h + '&lt;/svg&gt;';
  }
  /* Ağaç haritası (squarify) */
  function treemap(d, o) {
    o = o || {}; const W = o.W || 420, H = o.H || 230; const items = d.cats.map((c, i) =&gt; ({ c, v: d.series[0].vals[i], i })).filter(x =&gt; isNum(x.v) &amp;&amp; x.v &gt; 0).sort((a, b) =&gt; b.v - a.v);
    const tot = items.reduce((a, b) =&gt; a + b.v, 0) || 1; const rects = [];
    (function sq(list, x, y, w, h) {
      if (!list.length) return; if (list.length === 1) { rects.push([list[0], x, y, w, h]); return; }
      const sum = list.reduce((a, b) =&gt; a + b.v, 0); let half = 0, k = 0; while (k &lt; list.length - 1 &amp;&amp; half + list[k].v &lt; sum / 2) { half += list[k].v; k++; } if (k === 0) { half = list[0].v; k = 1; }
      const a = list.slice(0, k), b = list.slice(k); const f = half / sum;
      if (w &gt;= h) { sq(a, x, y, w * f, h); sq(b, x + w * f, y, w * (1 - f), h); } else { sq(a, x, y, w, h * f); sq(b, x, y + h * f, w, h * (1 - f)); }
    })(items, 0, 0, W, H);
    let h = svgOpen(W, H, o.label);
    rects.forEach(([it, x, y, w, hh]) =&gt; { h += '&lt;g class="vmark' + selCls(o, it.i) + '" data-i="' + it.i + '"' + tip(it.c, [[d.series[0].name, fmt(it.v, d.fmt)], ['Pay', fmt(it.v / tot, 'yuzde')]]) + ' tabindex="0"&gt;&lt;rect x="' + (x + 1) + '" y="' + (y + 1) + '" width="' + Math.max(0, w - 2) + '" height="' + Math.max(0, hh - 2) + '" rx="3" fill="' + S(it.i) + '"/&gt;' + (w &gt; 56 &amp;&amp; hh &gt; 30 ? txt(x + 8, y + 18, trunc(it.c, Math.floor(w / 7)), 'vt vtm') + txt(x + 8, y + 33, fmt(it.v, d.fmt, true), 'vt vtm2') : '') + '&lt;/g&gt;'; });
    return h + '&lt;/svg&gt;';
  }
  /* Dağılım */
  function scatter(pts, o) {
    o = o || {}; const W = o.W || 420, H = o.H || 250, padL = 52, padR = 14, padT = 12, padB = 40;
    const xs = pts.map(p =&gt; p.x).filter(isNum), ys = pts.map(p =&gt; p.y).filter(isNum); if (!xs.length) return '&lt;div class="empty"&gt;Veri yok&lt;/div&gt;';
    let tx = niceTicks(Math.min(0, ...xs), Math.max(...xs), 4), ty = niceTicks(Math.min(0, ...ys), Math.max(...ys), 4);
    const X = v =&gt; padL + (v - tx[0]) / (tx[tx.length - 1] - tx[0]) * (W - padL - padR), Y = v =&gt; padT + (H - padT - padB) - (v - ty[0]) / (ty[ty.length - 1] - ty[0]) * (H - padT - padB);
    let h = svgOpen(W, H, o.label);
    ty.forEach(t =&gt; { h += '&lt;line x1="' + padL + '" x2="' + (W - padR) + '" y1="' + Y(t) + '" y2="' + Y(t) + '" class="vgrid"/&gt;' + txt(padL - 6, Y(t) + 3.5, fmt(t, o.yfmt, true), 'vt vax', 'end'); });
    tx.forEach(t =&gt; { h += txt(X(t), H - padB + 15, fmt(t, o.xfmt, true), 'vt vax', 'middle'); });
    h += txt(W - padR, H - 6, o.xlabel || '', 'vt vax', 'end') + txt(padL, padT - 2, '', 'vt vax');
    const groups = [...new Set(pts.map(p =&gt; p.g))];
    pts.forEach((p, i) =&gt; { if (!isNum(p.x) || !isNum(p.y)) return; const gi = groups.indexOf(p.g); h += '&lt;circle class="vmark' + selCls(o, i) + '" data-i="' + i + '" cx="' + X(p.x) + '" cy="' + Y(p.y) + '" r="' + (p.r || 5) + '" fill="' + S(gi) + '" fill-opacity=".85" stroke="var(--surface)" stroke-width="1.5"' + tip(p.label, [[o.xlabel || 'X', fmt(p.x, o.xfmt)], [o.ylabel || 'Y', fmt(p.y, o.yfmt)]].concat(p.g ? [['Grup', p.g]] : [])) + ' tabindex="0"/&gt;'; });
    return h + '&lt;/svg&gt;' + (groups.length &gt; 1 ? legend(groups.map(g =&gt; ({ name: g }))) : '');
  }
  /* Tablo / matris (koşullu biçimlendirme ile) */
  function table(cols, rows, o) {
    o = o || {}; const fmts = o.fmts || [];
    const numCols = cols.map((c, j) =&gt; rows.some(r =&gt; isNum(r[j])));
    const ranges = cols.map((c, j) =&gt; { const v = rows.map(r =&gt; r[j]).filter(isNum); return v.length ? [Math.min(...v), Math.max(...v)] : null; });
    let h = '&lt;div class="vtable"&gt;&lt;table&gt;&lt;thead&gt;&lt;tr&gt;' + cols.map((c, j) =&gt; '&lt;th' + (numCols[j] ? ' class="n"' : '') + '&gt;' + esc(c) + '&lt;/th&gt;').join('') + '&lt;/tr&gt;&lt;/thead&gt;&lt;tbody&gt;';
    rows.forEach((r, i) =&gt; { h += '&lt;tr class="vmark' + selCls(o, i) + '" data-i="' + i + '"&gt;' + r.map((v, j) =&gt; { let style = ''; let inner = esc(fmt(v, fmts[j])); const cf = o.cf &amp;&amp; o.cf[j];
      if (cf &amp;&amp; isNum(v) &amp;&amp; ranges[j]) { const [a, b] = ranges[j]; const t = b &gt; a ? (v - a) / (b - a) : 1;
        if (cf === 'bar') inner = '&lt;span class="cfbar"&gt;&lt;i style="width:' + Math.max(2, t * 100) + '%"&gt;&lt;/i&gt;&lt;/span&gt;' + inner;
        if (cf === 'heat') style = ' style="background:color-mix(in srgb, var(--s1) ' + Math.round(8 + t * 52) + '%, transparent)"';
        if (cf === 'icon') inner = '&lt;span class="cfi ' + (v &gt;= (o.cfTarget || 0) ? 'ok' : 'bad') + '"&gt;' + (v &gt;= (o.cfTarget || 0) ? '▲' : '▼') + '&lt;/span&gt;' + inner; }
      return '&lt;td' + (numCols[j] ? ' class="n"' : '') + style + '&gt;' + inner + '&lt;/td&gt;'; }).join('') + '&lt;/tr&gt;'; });
    if (o.total) h += '&lt;tr class="tot"&gt;' + o.total.map((v, j) =&gt; '&lt;td' + (numCols[j] ? ' class="n"' : '') + '&gt;' + esc(j === 0 &amp;&amp; typeof v === 'string' ? v : fmt(v, fmts[j])) + '&lt;/td&gt;').join('') + '&lt;/tr&gt;';
    return h + '&lt;/tbody&gt;&lt;/table&gt;&lt;/div&gt;';
  }
  /* Dilimleyici */
  function slicer(id, label, values, selected) {
    return '&lt;div class="slicer"&gt;&lt;span class="eyebrow"&gt;' + esc(label) + '&lt;/span&gt;&lt;div class="sl-opts"&gt;' + values.map(v =&gt; '&lt;button data-slicer="' + esc(id) + '" data-v="' + esc(JSON.stringify(v)) + '" aria-pressed="' + (selected &amp;&amp; selected.some(s =&gt; s === v)) + '"&gt;' + esc(fmt(v)) + '&lt;/button&gt;').join('') + '&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;';
  }
  /* Küçük örnek resimler (görsel galerisi kartları için) */
  function thumb(type) {
    const g = 'var(--s1)', o2 = 'var(--s2)', a3 = 'var(--s3)';
    const b = (s) =&gt; '&lt;svg viewBox="0 0 80 50" class="vthumb" aria-hidden="true"&gt;' + s + '&lt;/svg&gt;';
    switch (type) {
      case 'column': return b('&lt;rect x="8" y="22" width="10" height="24" rx="2" fill="' + g + '"/&gt;&lt;rect x="24" y="12" width="10" height="34" rx="2" fill="' + g + '"/&gt;&lt;rect x="40" y="28" width="10" height="18" rx="2" fill="' + g + '"/&gt;&lt;rect x="56" y="6" width="10" height="40" rx="2" fill="' + g + '"/&gt;');
      case 'bar': return b('&lt;rect x="6" y="6" width="62" height="8" rx="2" fill="' + g + '"/&gt;&lt;rect x="6" y="18" width="46" height="8" rx="2" fill="' + g + '"/&gt;&lt;rect x="6" y="30" width="30" height="8" rx="2" fill="' + g + '"/&gt;&lt;rect x="6" y="42" width="16" height="6" rx="2" fill="' + g + '"/&gt;');
      case 'line': return b('&lt;path d="M4 40 L20 30 L36 34 L52 16 L76 10" stroke="' + g + '" stroke-width="2.5" fill="none"/&gt;&lt;path d="M4 44 L20 38 L36 40 L52 30 L76 26" stroke="' + o2 + '" stroke-width="2" fill="none" stroke-dasharray="4 3"/&gt;');
      case 'area': return b('&lt;path d="M4 40 L20 30 L36 34 L52 16 L76 10 L76 48 L4 48Z" fill="' + g + '" opacity=".25"/&gt;&lt;path d="M4 40 L20 30 L36 34 L52 16 L76 10" stroke="' + g + '" stroke-width="2.5" fill="none"/&gt;');
      case 'donut': return b('&lt;circle cx="40" cy="25" r="17" fill="none" stroke="' + g + '" stroke-width="9" stroke-dasharray="55 107"/&gt;&lt;circle cx="40" cy="25" r="17" fill="none" stroke="' + o2 + '" stroke-width="9" stroke-dasharray="30 107" stroke-dashoffset="-56"/&gt;&lt;circle cx="40" cy="25" r="17" fill="none" stroke="' + a3 + '" stroke-width="9" stroke-dasharray="20 107" stroke-dashoffset="-87"/&gt;');
      case 'card': return b('&lt;text x="40" y="28" text-anchor="middle" font-size="16" font-weight="700" fill="var(--ink)"&gt;1,2 Mn&lt;/text&gt;&lt;text x="40" y="42" text-anchor="middle" font-size="7" fill="var(--muted)"&gt;Net Satış&lt;/text&gt;');
      case 'kpi': return b('&lt;text x="40" y="24" text-anchor="middle" font-size="14" font-weight="700" fill="var(--ink)"&gt;747 B&lt;/text&gt;&lt;rect x="20" y="31" width="40" height="11" rx="5.5" fill="var(--ok-soft)"/&gt;&lt;text x="40" y="39.5" text-anchor="middle" font-size="7" fill="var(--ok)"&gt;▲ +4%&lt;/text&gt;');
      case 'gauge': return b('&lt;path d="M14 42 A26 26 0 0 1 66 42" stroke="var(--grid)" stroke-width="9" fill="none"/&gt;&lt;path d="M14 42 A26 26 0 0 1 56 22" stroke="' + g + '" stroke-width="9" fill="none"/&gt;&lt;line x1="60" y1="20" x2="66" y2="14" stroke="var(--ink)" stroke-width="2"/&gt;');
      case 'combo': return b('&lt;rect x="8" y="24" width="10" height="22" rx="2" fill="' + g + '"/&gt;&lt;rect x="24" y="16" width="10" height="30" rx="2" fill="' + g + '"/&gt;&lt;rect x="40" y="26" width="10" height="20" rx="2" fill="' + g + '"/&gt;&lt;rect x="56" y="10" width="10" height="36" rx="2" fill="' + g + '"/&gt;&lt;path d="M13 20 L29 18 L45 20 L61 14" stroke="' + o2 + '" stroke-width="2.5" fill="none"/&gt;');
      case 'stacked': return b('&lt;rect x="8" y="22" width="12" height="12" fill="' + g + '"/&gt;&lt;rect x="8" y="35" width="12" height="11" fill="' + o2 + '"/&gt;&lt;rect x="26" y="10" width="12" height="20" fill="' + g + '"/&gt;&lt;rect x="26" y="31" width="12" height="15" fill="' + o2 + '"/&gt;&lt;rect x="44" y="26" width="12" height="10" fill="' + g + '"/&gt;&lt;rect x="44" y="37" width="12" height="9" fill="' + o2 + '"/&gt;&lt;rect x="62" y="6" width="12" height="22" fill="' + g + '"/&gt;&lt;rect x="62" y="29" width="12" height="17" fill="' + o2 + '"/&gt;');
      case 'pct': return b('&lt;rect x="6" y="6" width="40" height="9" fill="' + g + '"/&gt;&lt;rect x="47" y="6" width="27" height="9" fill="' + o2 + '"/&gt;&lt;rect x="6" y="20" width="26" height="9" fill="' + g + '"/&gt;&lt;rect x="33" y="20" width="41" height="9" fill="' + o2 + '"/&gt;&lt;rect x="6" y="34" width="52" height="9" fill="' + g + '"/&gt;&lt;rect x="59" y="34" width="15" height="9" fill="' + o2 + '"/&gt;');
      case 'waterfall': return b('&lt;rect x="6" y="20" width="10" height="26" fill="' + g + '"/&gt;&lt;rect x="20" y="12" width="10" height="8" fill="var(--ok)"/&gt;&lt;rect x="34" y="12" width="10" height="12" fill="var(--err)"/&gt;&lt;rect x="48" y="6" width="10" height="18" fill="var(--ok)"/&gt;&lt;rect x="62" y="6" width="10" height="40" fill="' + g + '"/&gt;');
      case 'funnel': return b('&lt;rect x="6" y="4" width="68" height="9" rx="2" fill="' + g + '"/&gt;&lt;rect x="16" y="16" width="48" height="9" rx="2" fill="' + g + '" opacity=".85"/&gt;&lt;rect x="24" y="28" width="32" height="9" rx="2" fill="' + g + '" opacity=".7"/&gt;&lt;rect x="32" y="40" width="16" height="8" rx="2" fill="' + g + '" opacity=".55"/&gt;');
      case 'treemap': return b('&lt;rect x="4" y="4" width="40" height="42" rx="2" fill="' + g + '"/&gt;&lt;rect x="46" y="4" width="30" height="24" rx="2" fill="' + o2 + '"/&gt;&lt;rect x="46" y="30" width="16" height="16" rx="2" fill="' + a3 + '"/&gt;&lt;rect x="64" y="30" width="12" height="16" rx="2" fill="var(--s4)"/&gt;');
      case 'scatter': return b('&lt;circle cx="14" cy="36" r="3.5" fill="' + g + '"/&gt;&lt;circle cx="26" cy="28" r="3.5" fill="' + g + '"/&gt;&lt;circle cx="36" cy="32" r="3.5" fill="' + o2 + '"/&gt;&lt;circle cx="48" cy="18" r="3.5" fill="' + g + '"/&gt;&lt;circle cx="60" cy="22" r="3.5" fill="' + o2 + '"/&gt;&lt;circle cx="70" cy="10" r="3.5" fill="' + g + '"/&gt;');

  // araç ipucu
  let tipEl = null;
  function bindTips(rootEl) {
    if (!tipEl) { tipEl = document.createElement('div'); tipEl.className = 'vtip'; tipEl.hidden = true; document.body.appendChild(tipEl); }
    const show = (el, x, y) =&gt; { let d; try { d = JSON.parse(el.getAttribute('data-tip')); } catch (e) { return; } tipEl.innerHTML = '&lt;b&gt;' + esc(d.h) + '&lt;/b&gt;' + d.r.map(r =&gt; '&lt;span&gt;&lt;i&gt;' + esc(r[0]) + '&lt;/i&gt;' + esc(r[1]) + '&lt;/span&gt;').join(''); tipEl.hidden = false; const w = tipEl.offsetWidth, hh = tipEl.offsetHeight; tipEl.style.left = Math.max(8, Math.min(window.innerWidth - w - 8, x + 14)) + 'px'; tipEl.style.top = Math.max(8, y - hh - 12 &lt; 8 ? y + 18 : y - hh - 12) + 'px'; };
    rootEl.addEventListener('pointermove', e =&gt; { const el = e.target.closest('[data-tip]'); if (el &amp;&amp; rootEl.contains(el)) show(el, e.clientX, e.clientY); else tipEl.hidden = true; });
    rootEl.addEventListener('pointerleave', () =&gt; { tipEl.hidden = true; });
    rootEl.addEventListener('focusin', e =&gt; { const el = e.target.closest('[data-tip]'); if (el) { const r = el.getBoundingClientRect(); show(el, r.left + r.width / 2, r.top); } });
    rootEl.addEventListener('focusout', () =&gt; { tipEl.hidden = true; });
    window.addEventListener('scroll', () =&gt; { if (tipEl) tipEl.hidden = true; }, { passive: true });
  }
  root.PBIViz = { column, bar, line, donut, card, kpi, gauge, waterfall, funnel, treemap, scatter, table, slicer, thumb, fmt, bindTips, esc };
})(typeof window !== 'undefined' ? window : globalThis);

&lt;h2&gt;Alan kutuları: görselin iskeleti&lt;/h2&gt;

&lt;div class="tip"&gt;&lt;b&gt;Kural&lt;/b&gt;Eksene &lt;b&gt;sütun&lt;/b&gt; (gruplanacak şey), değerlere &lt;b&gt;ölçü&lt;/b&gt; (hesaplanacak şey) konur. &lt;h2&gt;Şerit (ribbon) sekmeleri&lt;/h2&gt;
&lt;ul&gt;
&lt;li&gt;&lt;b&gt;Giriş:&lt;/b&gt; Veri al, Verileri dönüştür (Power Query'yi açar), Yenile, Yayımla.&lt;/li&gt;

&lt;li&gt;&lt;b&gt;Değerleri değiştir:&lt;/b&gt; “istanbul” → “İstanbul”. Türkçe büyük İ/ı harflerine dikkat.&lt;/li&gt;

&lt;h2&gt;Yıldız şema&lt;/h2&gt;
&lt;p&gt;Olgu tablosu ortada durur, boyutlar çevresinde dizilir. Her boyut olguya &lt;b&gt;bire-çok (1:*)&lt;/b&gt; ilişkiyle bağlanır: bir ürünün çok satış satırı vardır. Diyagram bir yıldıza benzer. &lt;b&gt;Veri Modeli&lt;/b&gt; sayfasında bu modelin diyagramını görebilirsin.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;div class="tip"&gt;&lt;b&gt;Neden yıldız?&lt;/b&gt;Filtreler boyuttan olguya doğru akar. Kullanıcı dilimleyicide “Kitap” seçer → &lt;code&gt;Urunler&lt;/code&gt; süzülür → ilişki üzerinden &lt;code&gt;Satislar&lt;/code&gt; yalnızca kitap satırlarına iner → ölçü bu satırlar üzerinde hesaplanır. Tablolar az, ilişkiler net olduğunda hem DAX basitleşir hem motor hızlanır.&lt;/div&gt;
&lt;h2&gt;İlişkinin özellikleri&lt;/h2&gt;
&lt;ul&gt;
&lt;li&gt;&lt;b&gt;Kardinalite:&lt;/b&gt; &lt;i&gt;Bire-çok&lt;/i&gt; (en yaygın ve önerilen), &lt;i&gt;bire-bir&lt;/i&gt; (genelde iki tablonun birleştirilmesi gerektiğine işaret), &lt;i&gt;çoka-çok&lt;/i&gt; (dikkatli kullanılmalı).&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;&lt;b&gt;Çapraz filtre yönü:&lt;/b&gt; &lt;i&gt;Tek&lt;/i&gt; (boyuttan olguya; varsayılan ve önerilen) ya da &lt;i&gt;Her ikisi&lt;/i&gt; (çift yönlü; belirsizlik ve performans sorunu yaratabilir).&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;&lt;b&gt;Etkin / pasif:&lt;/b&gt; İki tablo arasında yalnızca bir ilişki etkin olabilir. Pasif ilişkiler kesik çizgiyle görünür ve DAX'ta &lt;code&gt;USERELATIONSHIP&lt;/code&gt; ile çağrılır (ör. Sipariş Tarihi ve Teslim Tarihi).&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;/ul&gt;
&lt;h2&gt;Anahtarlar&lt;/h2&gt;
&lt;p&gt;Boyut tablosundaki anahtar (&lt;code&gt;Urunler[UrunNo]&lt;/code&gt;) &lt;b&gt;benzersiz&lt;/b&gt; olmalıdır. Olgu tablosundaki yabancı anahtar (&lt;code&gt;Satislar[UrunNo]&lt;/code&gt;) tekrar edebilir. Boyut anahtarında tekrar varsa Power BI ilişkiyi çoka-çok yapar; bu genellikle veride bir sorun olduğunu gösterir.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;h2&gt;Kaçınılacak desenler&lt;/h2&gt;
&lt;ul&gt;
&lt;li&gt;&lt;b&gt;Tek dev tablo:&lt;/b&gt; Her şeyi tek tabloya birleştirmek ilk başta kolay gelir ama DAX'ı zorlaştırır ve modeli şişirir.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;&lt;b&gt;Kar tanesi (snowflake) aşırılığı:&lt;/b&gt; Boyutu boyuta, onu da başka boyuta bağlamak. Mümkünse Power Query'de boyutları düzleştir. (Bu modeldeki &lt;code&gt;Kategoriler&lt;/code&gt; bilinçli bir istisnadır: hem ürünleri hem hedefleri bağlayan ortak bir boyut.)&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;&lt;b&gt;Gereksiz çift yönlü ilişkiler.&lt;/b&gt;&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;/ul&gt;

  body: `
&lt;h2&gt;Neden ayrı bir tarih tablosu?&lt;/h2&gt;
&lt;ul&gt;
&lt;li&gt;&lt;b&gt;Zaman zekâsı fonksiyonları&lt;/b&gt; (&lt;code&gt;TOTALYTD&lt;/code&gt;, &lt;code&gt;SAMEPERIODLASTYEAR&lt;/code&gt;…) kesintisiz bir tarih sütunu ister: aralığın her günü bir kez bulunmalı.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Satış olmayan günler de raporda görünebilir (ör. “bu ay satış yok” sıfırı).&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Yıl, Çeyrek, Ay, Hafta günü gibi sütunlar tek yerde tanımlanır; &lt;code&gt;Satislar&lt;/code&gt; ve &lt;code&gt;Hedefler&lt;/code&gt; aynı takvimi paylaşır.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;/ul&gt;
&lt;h2&gt;DAX ile oluşturma&lt;/h2&gt;
&lt;p&gt;Modelleme → &lt;b&gt;Yeni tablo&lt;/b&gt;:&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;pre class="dax"&gt;Tarih =
ADDCOLUMNS(
    CALENDAR(DATE(2022, 1, 1), DATE(2024, 12, 31)),
    "Yil", YEAR([Date]),
    "AyNo", MONTH([Date]),
    "Ay", FORMAT([Date], "MMM"),
    "Ceyrek", "Ç" &amp;amp; QUARTER([Date])

&lt;p&gt;&lt;code&gt;CALENDAR&lt;/code&gt; iki tarih arasındaki her günü tek sütunlu bir tablo olarak döndürür. &lt;code&gt;ADDCOLUMNS&lt;/code&gt; bu tabloya yeni sütunlar ekler. Bu uygulamadaki &lt;code&gt;Tarih&lt;/code&gt; tablosu aynı mantıkla kuruldu.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;h2&gt;Kurulumdan sonra üç ayar&lt;/h2&gt;
&lt;ol&gt;
&lt;li&gt;&lt;b&gt;Tarih tablosu olarak işaretle:&lt;/b&gt; Tabloya sağ tık → &lt;i&gt;Tarih tablosu olarak işaretle&lt;/i&gt; → tarih sütununu seç. Böylece zaman zekâsı doğru çalışır.&lt;/li&gt;

&lt;/ol&gt;
&lt;p&gt;Bu filtrelerin tümüne &lt;b&gt;filtre bağlamı&lt;/b&gt; denir. Formül hiç değişmez; değişen, formülün gördüğü satırlardır. Toplam satırında kategori filtresi yoktur, bu yüzden toplam tüm kategorileri kapsar.&lt;/p&gt;

&lt;h2&gt;Satır bağlamı: “Şu an hangi satırdayım?”&lt;/h2&gt;

&lt;p&gt;&lt;code&gt;SUMX&lt;/code&gt;, &lt;code&gt;AVERAGEX&lt;/code&gt;, &lt;code&gt;MINX&lt;/code&gt;, &lt;code&gt;MAXX&lt;/code&gt;, &lt;code&gt;COUNTX&lt;/code&gt;, &lt;code&gt;CONCATENATEX&lt;/code&gt; aynı kalıbı izler:&lt;/p&gt;

&lt;h2&gt;Sorguları birleştir (Merge)&lt;/h2&gt;
&lt;p&gt;İki tabloyu ortak bir sütun üzerinden yan yana getirir; SQL'deki JOIN'in karşılığıdır. Giriş → &lt;b&gt;Sorguları Birleştir&lt;/b&gt;.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;div class="tbl-wrap"&gt;&lt;table&gt;

&lt;/table&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
&lt;p&gt;Birleştirmeden sonra yeni bir “tablo” sütunu oluşur; başlığındaki genişlet düğmesiyle istediğin sütunları seçersin.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;h2&gt;Sorguları ekle (Append)&lt;/h2&gt;
&lt;p&gt;Aynı yapıdaki tabloları alt alta ekler (SQL'de UNION ALL). Her ay ayrı dosya gelen satış raporları için idealdir. Daha da iyisi: &lt;b&gt;Klasör&lt;/b&gt; bağlayıcısı, bir klasördeki tüm dosyaları tek seferde birleştirir; klasöre yeni dosya eklendikçe yenilemede kendiliğinden gelir.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;h2&gt;Özetten çıkar (Unpivot)&lt;/h2&gt;
&lt;p&gt;Excel'den gelen “Ocak | Şubat | Mart …” sütunlu bütçe tablosunu modele uygun hâle getirir:&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;div class="tbl-wrap"&gt;&lt;table&gt;
&lt;tr&gt;&lt;th&gt;Kategori&lt;/th&gt;&lt;th&gt;Oca&lt;/th&gt;&lt;th&gt;Şub&lt;/th&gt;&lt;th&gt;&lt;/th&gt;&lt;th&gt;Kategori&lt;/th&gt;&lt;th&gt;Ay&lt;/th&gt;&lt;th&gt;Hedef&lt;/th&gt;&lt;/tr&gt;
&lt;tr&gt;&lt;td&gt;Kitap&lt;/td&gt;&lt;td&gt;4.000&lt;/td&gt;&lt;td&gt;4.200&lt;/td&gt;&lt;td&gt;→&lt;/td&gt;&lt;td&gt;Kitap&lt;/td&gt;&lt;td&gt;Oca&lt;/td&gt;&lt;td&gt;4.000&lt;/td&gt;&lt;/tr&gt;
&lt;tr&gt;&lt;td&gt;Spor&lt;/td&gt;&lt;td&gt;6.500&lt;/td&gt;&lt;td&gt;6.100&lt;/td&gt;&lt;td&gt;&lt;/td&gt;&lt;td&gt;Kitap&lt;/td&gt;&lt;td&gt;Şub&lt;/td&gt;&lt;td&gt;4.200&lt;/td&gt;&lt;/tr&gt;
&lt;/table&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
&lt;p&gt;Kategori sütununu seç → Dönüştür → &lt;b&gt;Diğer Sütunların Özetini Kaldır&lt;/b&gt;. Bu modeldeki &lt;code&gt;Hedefler&lt;/code&gt; tablosu tam olarak bu biçimdedir.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;h2&gt;Gruplandırma ve koşullu sütun&lt;/h2&gt;
&lt;ul&gt;
&lt;li&gt;&lt;b&gt;Gruplandırma ölçütü:&lt;/b&gt; Satırları özetler (ör. müşteri başına sipariş sayısı). Modele daha küçük bir tablo yüklemek için kullanılır.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;&lt;b&gt;Koşullu sütun:&lt;/b&gt; Sihirbazla “Eğer Fiyat &amp;gt; 1000 ise Pahalı” gibi kurallar. Hesaplanmış sütun yerine burada oluşturmak modeli verimli tutar.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;&lt;b&gt;Örneklerden sütun:&lt;/b&gt; Birkaç örnek yazarsın, Power Query kuralı kendisi çıkarır.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;/ul&gt;
&lt;h2&gt;Parametreler ve sorgu katlama&lt;/h2&gt;

&lt;li&gt;&lt;b&gt;Kart (yeni):&lt;/b&gt; Birden çok ölçüyü tek görselde, referans etiketleriyle gösterir.&lt;/li&gt;

{
  id: 'hesaplama-gruplari', lv: 'ileri', mod: 'İleri Modelleme', min: 14,

&lt;h2&gt;Ölçü patlaması&lt;/h2&gt;
&lt;p&gt;5 temel ölçün (Satış, Kâr, Adet, Sipariş, Müşteri) ve 6 zaman varyasyonun (Cari, GY, YTD, GY YTD, YoY, YoY %) varsa 30 ölçü yazman gerekir. &lt;b&gt;Hesaplama grupları&lt;/b&gt; bu varyasyonları bir kez tanımlar ve her ölçüye uygular.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;pre class="dax"&gt;-- Hesaplama grubu: Zaman Zekâsı, öğeler:
Cari    = SELECTEDMEASURE()
GY      = CALCULATE(SELECTEDMEASURE(), SAMEPERIODLASTYEAR(Tarih[Tarih]))
YTD     = CALCULATE(SELECTEDMEASURE(), DATESYTD(Tarih[Tarih]))
YoY %   = VAR c = SELECTEDMEASURE()
          VAR g = CALCULATE(SELECTEDMEASURE(), SAMEPERIODLASTYEAR(Tarih[Tarih]))

&lt;p&gt;&lt;code&gt;SELECTEDMEASURE()&lt;/code&gt;, görselde o an kullanılan ölçünün yerine geçer. Hesaplama grubu modelde bir tablo gibi görünür; öğelerini matrisin sütununa koyduğunda her ölçü için altı varyasyon kendiliğinden gelir. Öğeler &lt;b&gt;biçim dizesi ifadesi&lt;/b&gt; de taşıyabilir: YoY % öğesi sonucu yüzde olarak gösterir.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;Desktop'ta Model görünümünde &lt;b&gt;Hesaplama grubu&lt;/b&gt; düğmesiyle (ya da Tabular Editor ile) oluşturulur. Hesaplama grubu eklenince örtük ölçüler kapanır; tüm değerler açık ölçü olmalıdır.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;h2&gt;Alan parametreleri&lt;/h2&gt;
&lt;p&gt;Modelleme → Yeni parametre → &lt;b&gt;Alanlar&lt;/b&gt;. Kullanıcıya bir dilimleyiciden “Grafiğin ekseni: Kategori / Kanal / Segment” ya da “Değer: Satış / Kâr / Adet” seçtirirsin. Arka planda özel bir tablo ve &lt;code&gt;NAMEOF&lt;/code&gt; fonksiyonu kullanılır.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;h2&gt;Görsel hesaplamalar&lt;/h2&gt;
&lt;p&gt;Görselin kendisinde, yalnızca görseldeki verilerle çalışan DAX hesapları. Model ölçüsü yazmadan kümülatif toplam, hareketli ortalama, önceki satıra göre fark:&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;pre class="dax"&gt;Kümülatif = RUNNINGSUM([Net Satış])
Öncekine Göre = [Net Satış] - PREVIOUS([Net Satış])&lt;/pre&gt;
&lt;p&gt;Görsel hesaplamalar matris ızgarası üzerinde düşünür (satırlar, sütunlar, üst düzey). Model ölçülerinden daha basit yazılır ama yalnızca o görselde yaşar.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;div class="note"&gt;&lt;b&gt;Bu oyun alanında&lt;/b&gt;Hesaplama grupları ve görsel hesaplamalar model ya da görsel düzeyinde tanımlandığı için DAX Stüdyosu'nda çalıştırılamaz. Kavramları ve kalıpları bilmen sınav ve iş için yeterlidir.&lt;/div&gt;

&lt;h2&gt;RLS: herkes kendi verisini görsün&lt;/h2&gt;
&lt;p&gt;&lt;b&gt;Satır düzeyinde güvenlik&lt;/b&gt; (Row-Level Security), aynı raporu açan kişilerin farklı satırları görmesini sağlar. Modelleme → &lt;b&gt;Rolleri yönet&lt;/b&gt;.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;h3&gt;Statik RLS&lt;/h3&gt;
&lt;pre class="dax"&gt;-- "İstanbul Ekibi" rolü, Musteriler tablosu filtresi:
Musteriler[Sehir] = "İstanbul"&lt;/pre&gt;
&lt;p&gt;Rolün üyeleri yalnızca İstanbul'daki müşterileri ve (ilişki üzerinden) onların satışlarını görür. Her şehir için ayrı rol gerekir.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;h3&gt;Dinamik RLS&lt;/h3&gt;
&lt;p&gt;Bir &lt;code&gt;Yetkiler&lt;/code&gt; tablosu kur: &lt;code&gt;Eposta | Sehir&lt;/code&gt;. Tek bir rol yeter:&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;pre class="dax"&gt;-- Yetkiler tablosu filtresi:
Yetkiler[Eposta] = USERPRINCIPALNAME()&lt;/pre&gt;
&lt;p&gt;&lt;code&gt;USERPRINCIPALNAME()&lt;/code&gt;, oturum açan kişinin e-postasını döndürür. Yeni bir çalışan geldiğinde rol değişmez, yalnızca tabloya bir satır eklenir.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;h2&gt;Test et&lt;/h2&gt;

&lt;h2&gt;OLS: sütunu ya da tabloyu gizle&lt;/h2&gt;
&lt;p&gt;&lt;b&gt;Nesne düzeyinde güvenlik&lt;/b&gt; (Object-Level Security), belirli rollerden bir tabloyu ya da sütunu (ör. &lt;code&gt;Calisanlar[Maas]&lt;/code&gt;) tamamen gizler. Tabular Editor gibi araçlarla tanımlanır.&lt;/p&gt;

&lt;li&gt;RLS, çalışma alanında &lt;b&gt;Görüntüleyici&lt;/b&gt; rolündeki kullanıcılara uygulanır. Yönetici, Üye ve Katkıda bulunan rolleri modeli düzenleyebildiği için RLS'den etkilenmez.&lt;/li&gt;

&lt;li&gt;RLS rapor sayfasını gizlemek için değil, &lt;b&gt;veriyi&lt;/b&gt; korumak içindir. Sayfa gizleme bir güvenlik önlemi değildir.&lt;/li&gt;

&lt;h2&gt;Özel fonksiyon&lt;/h2&gt;
&lt;pre class="m"&gt;let
    TemizSehir = (s as nullable text) as nullable text =&amp;gt;
        if s = null then null
        else Text.Proper(Text.Trim(Text.Replace(s, "i̇", "i")))
in
    TemizSehir&lt;/pre&gt;

function hl(src) {
  const re = /(\/\/[^\n]*|--[^\n]*|\/\*[\s\S]*?(?:\*\/|$))|("(?:[^"]|"")*"?)|('[^'\n]*'?)(\[[^\]\n]*\]?)?|([A-Za-z_À-￿][\w.À-￿]*)(\[[^\]\n]*\]?)?|(\[[^\]\n]*\]?)|(\b\d+(?:\.\d+)?\b)|([\s\S])/g;
  let out = '', m;
  while ((m = re.exec(src))) {
    if (m[1]) out += '&lt;span class="c"&gt;' + esc(m[1]) + '&lt;/span&gt;';
    else if (m[2]) out += '&lt;span class="s"&gt;' + esc(m[2]) + '&lt;/span&gt;';
    else if (m[3]) out += '&lt;span class="v"&gt;' + esc(m[3]) + (m[4] ? esc(m[4]) : '') + '&lt;/span&gt;';
    else if (m[5]) { const w = m[5], up = w.toUpperCase(); if (m[6]) out += '&lt;span class="v"&gt;' + esc(w + m[6]) + '&lt;/span&gt;'; else if (KWS.has(up)) out += '&lt;span class="k"&gt;' + esc(w) + '&lt;/span&gt;'; else if (FNSET.has(up) &amp;&amp; /^\s*\(/.test(src.slice(re.lastIndex))) out += '&lt;span class="f"&gt;' + esc(w) + '&lt;/span&gt;'; else if (MODEL.tables[w]) out += '&lt;span class="v"&gt;' + esc(w) + '&lt;/span&gt;'; else out += esc(w); }
    else if (m[7]) out += '&lt;span class="ms"&gt;' + esc(m[7]) + '&lt;/span&gt;';
    else if (m[8]) out += '&lt;span class="n"&gt;' + m[8] + '&lt;/span&gt;';
    else out += esc(m[9]);
  }
  return out;
}

function renderStats() {
  const st = streak(), done = Object.keys(P.done).filter(k =&gt; LMAP[k]).length; const lv = myLevel();
  const ss = $('#sideStats'); if (ss) ss.innerHTML = '&lt;div class="stat"&gt;&lt;b&gt;' + P.xp + '&lt;/b&gt;&lt;span&gt;XP&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class="stat"&gt;&lt;b&gt;' + st + '&lt;/b&gt;&lt;span&gt;günlük seri&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class="stat"&gt;&lt;b&gt;' + done + '/' + LESSONS.length + '&lt;/b&gt;&lt;span&gt;ders&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class="stat"&gt;&lt;b&gt;' + (lv ? lvName(lv) : '—') + '&lt;/b&gt;&lt;span&gt;seviye&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;a class="sibling" href="' + SQL_URL + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener"&gt;SQLPatika\'ya geç ↗&lt;/a&gt;';
  const ms = $('#miniStats'); if (ms) ms.innerHTML = '&lt;b&gt;' + P.xp + '&lt;/b&gt;&amp;nbsp;XP · &lt;b&gt;' + st + '&lt;/b&gt;&amp;nbsp;gün';
}
const HASHV = { curr: 'dersler', model: 'model', studio: 'studyo', formulas: 'formuller', visuals: 'gorseller', dash: 'dashboard', test: 'test', dict: 'sozluk', paths: 'patikalar', more: 'menu' };
function go(view, id, opts) {
  opts = opts || {};
  if (view === 'path' &amp;&amp; !id) view = 'paths';
  S = { view, id: id || null, step: opts.step || 0, ctx: opts.ctx !== undefined ? opts.ctx : (view === 'lesson' ? S.ctx : null) };
  if (view === 'path') S.ctx = id;
  const hash = view === 'lesson' ? 'ders-' + id : view === 'path' ? 'patika-' + id : view === 'dash' &amp;&amp; id ? 'dashboard-' + id : view === 'home' ? '' : (HASHV[view] || view);
  try { history.replaceState(null, '', hash ? ('#' + hash) : location.pathname + location.search); } catch (e) {}
  render(); window.scrollTo(0, 0); const m = $('#main'); if (m) m.focus({ preventScroll: true });
}
function render() {
  renderNav(); renderStats();
  const v = S.view, main = $('#main');
  const views = { home: viewHome, paths: viewPaths, path: () =&gt; viewPath(S.id), curr: viewCurr, lesson: viewLesson, model: viewModel, studio: viewStudio, formulas: viewFormulas, visuals: viewVisuals, dash: viewDash, test: viewTest, dict: viewDict, more: viewMore };
  main.innerHTML = (views[v] || viewHome)();
  enhance(main);
  if (v === 'lesson') mountLesson();
  if (v === 'studio') mountStudio();
  if (v === 'dash') mountDash();
  if (v === 'model') mountModel();
  if (v === 'visuals') mountVisuals();
}
function enhance(root) {
  root.querySelectorAll('pre.dax').forEach(pre =&gt; {
    if (pre.dataset.done) return; pre.dataset.done = 1; const src = pre.textContent; pre.innerHTML = hl(src);
    const wrap = document.createElement('div'); wrap.className = 'codebox'; pre.parentNode.insertBefore(wrap, pre); wrap.appendChild(pre);
    if (!pre.hasAttribute('data-notry')) { const b = document.createElement('button'); b.className = 'try'; b.type = 'button'; b.textContent = 'Stüdyoda dene'; b.addEventListener('click', () =&gt; openInStudio(src)); wrap.appendChild(b); }
  });
  root.querySelectorAll('pre.m').forEach(pre =&gt; { if (pre.dataset.done) return; pre.dataset.done = 1; pre.innerHTML = hlM(pre.textContent); });
  root.querySelectorAll('.theory table').forEach(t =&gt; { if (t.parentNode.classList.contains('tbl-wrap')) return; const w = document.createElement('div'); w.className = 'tbl-wrap'; t.parentNode.insertBefore(w, t); w.appendChild(t); });
}
function openInStudio(src) {
  src = src.trim();
  // birden çok ölçü tanımı içeren bloklardan ilkini ya da son tanımı al
  const isQ = /^\s*(DEFINE|EVALUATE)\b/i.test(src) || /\bEVALUATE\b/.test(src);
  if (!isQ) {
    const defs = splitDefs(src);
    if (defs.length &gt; 1) { defs.slice(0, -1).forEach(d =&gt; { if (d.name &amp;&amp; !LIBMAP[d.name]) upsertMine(d.name, d.expr, true); }); src = defs[defs.length - 1].name ? defs[defs.length - 1].name + ' = ' + defs[defs.length - 1].expr : defs[defs.length - 1].expr; }
  }
  ST.mode = isQ ? 'q' : 'm'; ST.code[ST.mode] = src; saveStudio(); go('studio');
}
function splitDefs(src) {
  const lines = src.split('\n'); const out = []; let cur = null;
  lines.forEach(line =&gt; { const m = line.match(/^([^\s\-\/=()\[\]"'{},][^=()\[\]"{},]*?)\s*:?=(?!=)\s*(.*)$/); if (m &amp;&amp; !/^(VAR|RETURN)\b/i.test(m[1].trim())) { cur = { name: m[1].trim(), expr: m[2] }; out.push(cur); } else if (cur) cur.expr += '\n' + line; else { cur = { name: null, expr: line }; out.push(cur); } });
  return out.map(d =&gt; ({ name: d.name, expr: d.expr.replace(/^\s*\n/, '').replace(/\s+$/, '') })).filter(d =&gt; d.expr.trim() &amp;&amp; !/^--/.test(d.expr.trim()));
}

/* ---------- Ana sayfa ---------- */
function lessonRow(l, ctx) {
  const st = lessonState(l), n = (l.ex || []).length; const nd = (l.ex || []).filter(e =&gt; e.t !== 'mc').length;
  return '&lt;button class="litem" data-lesson="' + l.id + '"' + (ctx ? ' data-ctx="' + ctx + '"' : '') + '&gt;&lt;span class="check ' + (st === 'done' ? 'done' : st === 'part' ? 'part' : '') + '"&gt;' + (st === 'done' ? ic('tick', 3.2) : '') + '&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span style="min-width:0"&gt;&lt;b&gt;' + esc(l.title) + '&lt;/b&gt;&lt;small&gt;' + esc(l.sum || '') + '&lt;/small&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span class="row" style="gap:6px;flex-wrap:nowrap"&gt;' + (nd ? '&lt;span class="chip dx"&gt;' + nd + ' DAX&lt;/span&gt;' : '') + '&lt;span class="chip"&gt;' + (n ? n + ' alıştırma' : 'okuma') + '&lt;/span&gt;' + lvBadge(l.lv) + '&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/button&gt;';
}
function viewHome() {
  const st = streak(), td = P.days[today()] || 0, nx = (P.last &amp;&amp; LMAP[P.last] &amp;&amp; !P.done[P.last]) ? LMAP[P.last] : nextLesson(S.ctx);
  const done = Object.keys(P.done).filter(k =&gt; LMAP[k]).length, totalEx = LESSONS.reduce((a, l) =&gt; a + (l.ex || []).length, 0), daxEx = LESSONS.reduce((a, l) =&gt; a + (l.ex || []).filter(e =&gt; e.t !== 'mc').length, 0);
  let h = '&lt;div class="wrap"&gt;';

Konuyu oku, ölçüyü yaz, sonucu matris ve grafikte anında kontrol et. Örnek veri: SQLPatika\'daki Anadolu Market, Power BI\'ın sevdiği yıldız şemada.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;div class="row"&gt;';
  h += nx ? '&lt;button class="btn pri" data-lesson="' + nx.id + '"&gt;' + (done ? 'Devam et' : 'Hemen başla') + ': ' + esc(nx.title) + '&lt;/button&gt;' : '&lt;button class="btn pri" data-go="studio"&gt;DAX Stüdyosu\'nu aç&lt;/button&gt;';
  h += P.placement ? '&lt;button class="btn" data-go="paths"&gt;Patika seç&lt;/button&gt;' : '&lt;button class="btn" data-go="test"&gt;Seviyemi belirle&lt;/button&gt;';
  h += '&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;';

  h += '&lt;div class="kpis"&gt;&lt;div class="kpi"&gt;&lt;b&gt;' + P.xp + '&lt;/b&gt;&lt;span&gt;toplam XP&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class="kpi"&gt;&lt;b&gt;' + st + '&lt;/b&gt;&lt;span&gt;gün seri&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class="kpi"&gt;&lt;b&gt;' + done + '&lt;/b&gt;&lt;span&gt;biten ders&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;';
  if (nx) h += '&lt;div class="continue"&gt;' + lvBadge(nx.lv) + '&lt;div class="grow"&gt;&lt;b&gt;' + esc(nx.title) + '&lt;/b&gt;&lt;span class="muted" style="font-size:.85rem"&gt;' + esc(nx.mod) + ' · ~' + (nx.min || 10) + ' dk&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;button class="btn sm" data-lesson="' + nx.id + '"&gt;Aç&lt;/button&gt;&lt;/div&gt;';
  h += '&lt;span class="muted" style="font-size:.8rem"&gt;İlerlemen bu tarayıcıda saklanır.&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/section&gt;';
  // araçlar
  h += '&lt;section class="lvl-block"&gt;&lt;div class="sec-head"&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span class="eyebrow"&gt;Atölye&lt;/span&gt;&lt;h2&gt;Okumanın ötesi&lt;/h2&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class="tools"&gt;';
  [['studio', 'term', 'DAX Stüdyosu', 'Ölçü yaz, satır eksenini seç, matris ve grafikte gör. EVALUATE sorguları da çalışır.'], ['dash', 'dash', 'Dashboardlar', 'Dört hazır dashboard. Dilimleyicileri kullan, grafiklere tıkla, arkadaki DAX\'ı aç.'], ['visuals', 'chart', 'Görseller', LIB.length ? (window.PBI_VISUALS.length + ' görsel türü: ne zaman kullanılır, nelerden kaçınılır, canlı örnek.') : ''], ['formulas', 'fx', 'DAX Formülleri', window.PBI_CATALOG.length + ' fonksiyon: söz dizimi, açıklama ve tek tıkla denenebilir örnek.'], ['model', 'model', 'Veri Modeli', 'Yıldız şema diyagramı, tablolar, sütunlar ve ilk satırlar.']].forEach(([v, i, t, d]) =&gt; { h += '&lt;button class="tool" data-go="' + v + '"&gt;&lt;span class="tic"&gt;' + ic(i) + '&lt;/span&gt;&lt;b&gt;' + t + '&lt;/b&gt;&lt;small&gt;' + d + '&lt;/small&gt;&lt;/button&gt;'; });
  h += '&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/section&gt;';
  h += '&lt;section class="lvl-block"&gt;&lt;div class="sec-head"&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span class="eyebrow"&gt;Seviye haritası&lt;/span&gt;&lt;h2&gt;Temelden ileriye üç basamak&lt;/h2&gt;&lt;/div&gt;' + (myLevel() ? '&lt;span class="chip"&gt;Şu anki seviyen: ' + lvName(myLevel()) + '&lt;/span&gt;' : '') + '&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class="climb"&gt;';
  LEVELS.forEach(L =&gt; { const ids = levelIds(L.id), p = pct(ids); h += '&lt;button class="stop" data-go="curr" data-lv="' + L.id + '"&gt;&lt;span class="code" style="color:var(--' + L.c + ')"&gt;' + L.name + '&lt;/span&gt;&lt;small&gt;' + L.desc + '&lt;/small&gt;&lt;span class="muted num" style="font-size:.78rem"&gt;' + ids.filter(i =&gt; P.done[i]).length + '/' + ids.length + ' ders&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span class="bar"&gt;&lt;i style="width:' + p + '%;background:var(--' + L.c + ')"&gt;&lt;/i&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/button&gt;'; });
  h += '&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/section&gt;';
  h += '&lt;section class="lvl-block"&gt;&lt;div class="sec-head"&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span class="eyebrow"&gt;Kariyer patikaları&lt;/span&gt;&lt;h2&gt;Hedefine göre yol seç&lt;/h2&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;button class="btn sm ghost" data-go="paths"&gt;Tümünü gör&lt;/button&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class="paths"&gt;' + PATHS.map(pathCard).join('') + '&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/section&gt;';
  h += '&lt;section class="row" style="justify-content:space-between"&gt;&lt;span class="muted" style="font-size:.85rem"&gt;SQL tarafını da öğrenmek ister misin? Aynı veri &lt;a href="' + SQL_URL + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener"&gt;SQLPatika&lt;/a&gt;\'da SQL ile sorgulanıyor.&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span id="resetBox" class="confirm"&gt;&lt;button class="btn sm ghost" data-act="reset-ask"&gt;İlerlemeyi sıfırla&lt;/button&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/section&gt;';
  return h + '&lt;/div&gt;';
