function pathCard(p) {
  const ids = p.lessons.filter(id =&gt; LMAP[id]), pc = pct(ids);
  return '&lt;button class="pcard" data-go="path" data-id="' + p.id + '"&gt;&lt;span class="row" style="justify-content:space-between"&gt;&lt;span class="ic" style="background:' + p.color + '"&gt;' + p.short + '&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span class="chip"&gt;' + ids.length + ' ders&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;h3&gt;' + esc(p.name) + '&lt;/h3&gt;&lt;p&gt;' + esc(p.desc) + '&lt;/p&gt;&lt;span class="bar"&gt;&lt;i style="width:' + pc + '%;background:' + p.color + '"&gt;&lt;/i&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span class="foot"&gt;&lt;span&gt;%' + pc + ' tamamlandı&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;~' + Math.max(1, Math.round(ids.reduce((a, id) =&gt; a + (LMAP[id].min || 10), 0) / 60)) + ' saat&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/button&gt;';
}
function viewPaths() { return '&lt;div class="wrap"&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span class="eyebrow"&gt;Patikalar&lt;/span&gt;&lt;h1&gt;Hangi rol için öğreniyorsun?&lt;/h1&gt;&lt;p class="muted" style="max-width:62ch"&gt;Her patika, dersleri o role göre seçer ve sıralar. Ortak dersleri bir kez bitirdiğinde tüm patikalarda tamamlanmış sayılır.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class="paths"&gt;' + PATHS.map(pathCard).join('') + '&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;'; }
function viewPath(id) {
  const p = PMAP[id]; if (!p) return viewPaths();
  const ls = p.lessons.map(x =&gt; LMAP[x]).filter(Boolean), nx = ls.find(l =&gt; !P.done[l.id]);
  let h = '&lt;div class="wrap"&gt;&lt;div class="crumbs"&gt;&lt;button data-go="paths"&gt;Patikalar&lt;/button&gt;&lt;span&gt;/&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;' + esc(p.name) + '&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;';
  h += '&lt;section class="panel hero-main"&gt;&lt;div class="row"&gt;&lt;span class="ic" style="background:' + p.color + ';height:46px;min-width:46px"&gt;' + p.short + '&lt;/span&gt;&lt;div class="grow"&gt;&lt;span class="eyebrow"&gt;Kariyer patikası&lt;/span&gt;&lt;h1 style="font-size:1.9rem"&gt;' + esc(p.name) + '&lt;/h1&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;p&gt;' + esc(p.long || p.desc) + '&lt;/p&gt;';
  if (p.skills) h += '&lt;div class="row" style="gap:6px"&gt;' + p.skills.map(s =&gt; '&lt;span class="chip"&gt;' + esc(s) + '&lt;/span&gt;').join('') + '&lt;/div&gt;';
  h += '&lt;div class="bar"&gt;&lt;i style="width:' + pct(p.lessons) + '%;background:' + p.color + '"&gt;&lt;/i&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class="row"&gt;' + (nx ? '&lt;button class="btn pri" data-lesson="' + nx.id + '" data-ctx="' + p.id + '"&gt;' + (pct(p.lessons) ? 'Devam et' : 'Patikaya başla') + '&lt;/button&gt;' : '&lt;span class="chip"&gt;Patika tamamlandı&lt;/span&gt;') + '&lt;span class="muted num"&gt;' + ls.filter(l =&gt; P.done[l.id]).length + '/' + ls.length + ' ders · %' + pct(p.lessons) + '&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/section&gt;';
  LEVELS.forEach(L =&gt; { const items = ls.filter(l =&gt; l.lv === L.id); if (!items.length) return; h += '&lt;section class="lvl-block"&gt;&lt;div class="lvl-head"&gt;' + lvBadge(L.id) + '&lt;h2&gt;' + L.name + '&lt;/h2&gt;&lt;span class="muted" style="font-size:.88rem"&gt;' + items.length + ' ders&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class="panel lessons"&gt;' + items.map(l =&gt; lessonRow(l, p.id)).join('') + '&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/section&gt;'; });
  return h + '&lt;/div&gt;';
}
function viewMore() {
  return '&lt;div class="wrap"&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span class="eyebrow"&gt;Menü&lt;/span&gt;&lt;h1&gt;Tüm bölümler&lt;/h1&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class="tools"&gt;' + NAV.map(([v, t, i]) =&gt; '&lt;button class="tool" data-go="' + v + '"&gt;&lt;span class="tic"&gt;' + ic(i) + '&lt;/span&gt;&lt;b&gt;' + t + '&lt;/b&gt;&lt;/button&gt;').join('') + '&lt;a class="tool" href="' + SQL_URL + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener"&gt;&lt;span class="tic"&gt;' + ic('term') + '&lt;/span&gt;&lt;b&gt;SQLPatika ↗&lt;/b&gt;&lt;small&gt;Aynı veriyle SQL öğren&lt;/small&gt;&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;';
}

/* ---------- Müfredat ---------- */
let currFilter = { lv: 'all', q: '' };
function viewCurr() {
  return '&lt;div class="wrap"&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span class="eyebrow"&gt;Tüm dersler&lt;/span&gt;&lt;h1&gt;Müfredat&lt;/h1&gt;&lt;p class="muted" style="max-width:66ch"&gt;Dersler üç seviyede ve konu modüllerinde toplanır. İstediğin dersi açabilirsin; sıra yalnızca öneridir.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class="row"&gt;&lt;input class="input" id="currQ" placeholder="Ders ara: CALCULATE, zaman, RLS, Power Query…" value="' + esc(currFilter.q) + '" style="max-width:360px" aria-label="Ders ara"&gt;&lt;div class="filters"&gt;' + ['all'].concat(LEVELS.map(l =&gt; l.id)).map(x =&gt; '&lt;button data-lvf="' + x + '" aria-pressed="' + (currFilter.lv === x) + '"&gt;' + (x === 'all' ? 'Tümü' : lvName(x)) + '&lt;/button&gt;').join('') + '&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div id="currList"&gt;' + currList() + '&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;';
}
function currList() {
  const q = currFilter.q.trim().toLocaleLowerCase('tr'); let h = '';
  LEVELS.forEach(L =&gt; {
    if (currFilter.lv !== 'all' &amp;&amp; currFilter.lv !== L.id) return;
    const ls = LESSONS.filter(l =&gt; l.lv === L.id &amp;&amp; (!q || (l.title + ' ' + (l.sum || '') + ' ' + l.mod + ' ' + l.body).toLocaleLowerCase('tr').includes(q)));
    if (!ls.length) return;
    const mods = []; ls.forEach(l =&gt; { let m = mods.find(x =&gt; x.n === l.mod); if (!m) { m = { n: l.mod, items: [] }; mods.push(m); } m.items.push(l); });
    h += '&lt;section class="lvl-block" style="margin-bottom:28px"&gt;&lt;div class="lvl-head"&gt;' + lvBadge(L.id) + '&lt;h2&gt;' + L.name + '&lt;/h2&gt;&lt;span class="muted" style="font-size:.88rem"&gt;' + L.desc + '&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class="panel lessons"&gt;' + mods.map(m =&gt; '&lt;div class="mod-head"&gt;' + esc(m.n) + '&lt;/div&gt;' + m.items.map(l =&gt; lessonRow(l)).join('')).join('') + '&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/section&gt;';
  });
  return h || '&lt;div class="panel empty"&gt;Aramana uyan ders bulunamadı.&lt;/div&gt;';
}

/* ---------- Ders ---------- */
let ED = null;
function viewLesson() {
  const l = LMAP[S.id]; if (!l) return '&lt;div class="wrap"&gt;&lt;div class="panel empty"&gt;Ders bulunamadı.&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;';
  P.last = l.id; save(); const ex = l.ex || [];
  let h = '&lt;div class="wrap" style="gap:18px"&gt;&lt;div class="lhead"&gt;&lt;div class="crumbs"&gt;' + (S.ctx &amp;&amp; PMAP[S.ctx] ? '&lt;button data-go="path" data-id="' + S.ctx + '"&gt;' + esc(PMAP[S.ctx].name) + '&lt;/button&gt;' : '&lt;button data-go="curr"&gt;Dersler&lt;/button&gt;') + '&lt;span&gt;/&lt;/span&gt;' + lvBadge(l.lv) + '&lt;span&gt;' + esc(l.mod) + '&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;';
  h += '&lt;div class="row" style="justify-content:space-between;align-items:end"&gt;&lt;h1 style="font-size:clamp(1.5rem,3.4vw,2.1rem)"&gt;' + esc(l.title) + '&lt;/h1&gt;&lt;span class="muted" style="font-size:.85rem"&gt;~' + (l.min || 10) + ' dk&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;';
  h += '&lt;div class="steps"&gt;&lt;button data-step="0" aria-current="' + (S.step === 0) + '" class="' + (P.seen[l.id] ? 'done' : '') + '"&gt;Konu&lt;/button&gt;' + ex.map((e, i) =&gt; '&lt;button data-step="' + (i + 1) + '" aria-current="' + (S.step === i + 1) + '" class="' + (P.ex[exKey(l, i)] ? 'done' : '') + '" aria-label="Alıştırma ' + (i + 1) + '"&gt;' + (i + 1) + (e.t !== 'mc' ? '&lt;i class="dxdot" title="DAX"&gt;&lt;/i&gt;' : '') + '&lt;/button&gt;').join('') + '&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;';
  if (S.step === 0) {
    h += '&lt;article class="panel theory"&gt;' + l.body + '&lt;/article&gt;&lt;div class="pager"&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;' + (ex.length ? '&lt;button class="btn pri" data-step="1"&gt;Alıştırmalara geç (' + ex.length + ')&lt;/button&gt;' : (P.done[l.id] ? nextBtn(l) : '&lt;button class="btn pri" data-act="read-done"&gt;Okudum, dersi tamamla&lt;/button&gt;')) + '&lt;/div&gt;';
  } else if (S.step &lt;= ex.length) {
    const e = ex[S.step - 1], k = exKey(l, S.step - 1);
    h += e.t === 'mc' ? mcView(e, k) : daxView(e, k);
    h += '&lt;div class="pager"&gt;&lt;button class="btn" data-step="' + (S.step - 1) + '"&gt;Geri&lt;/button&gt;' + (S.step &lt; ex.length ? '&lt;button class="btn" data-step="' + (S.step + 1) + '"&gt;Sonraki alıştırma&lt;/button&gt;' : (P.done[l.id] ? nextBtn(l) : '&lt;button class="btn" data-step="0"&gt;Konuya dön&lt;/button&gt;')) + '&lt;/div&gt;';
  }
  return h + '&lt;/div&gt;';
}
function nextBtn(l) {
  let nx = null;
  if (S.ctx &amp;&amp; PMAP[S.ctx]) { const arr = PMAP[S.ctx].lessons; const i = arr.indexOf(l.id); nx = LMAP[arr.slice(i + 1).find(id =&gt; LMAP[id] &amp;&amp; !P.done[id]) || arr[i + 1]]; }
  else nx = LESSONS.slice(l._i + 1).find(x =&gt; !P.done[x.id]) || LESSONS[l._i + 1];
  return nx ? '&lt;button class="btn pri" data-lesson="' + nx.id + '"&gt;Sonraki ders: ' + esc(nx.title) + '&lt;/button&gt;' : '&lt;button class="btn pri" data-go="home"&gt;Ana sayfa&lt;/button&gt;';
}
function mcView(e, k) {
  const solved = P.ex[k];
  let h = '&lt;div class="panel brief" style="max-width:820px"&gt;&lt;span class="eyebrow"&gt;Soru' + (solved ? ' · çözüldü' : '') + '&lt;/span&gt;&lt;div class="q"&gt;' + e.q + '&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class="mc' + (e.thumbs ? ' mc-vis' : '') + '" id="mc"&gt;';
  e.o.forEach((o, i) =&gt; { h += '&lt;button data-mc="' + i + '" class="' + (solved &amp;&amp; i === e.a ? 'right' : '') + '"&gt;' + (e.thumbs ? V.thumb(e.thumbs[i]) : '&lt;span class="key"&gt;' + String.fromCharCode(65 + i) + '&lt;/span&gt;') + '&lt;span&gt;' + o + '&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/button&gt;'; });
  return h + '&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div id="mcFb"&gt;' + (solved &amp;&amp; e.why ? '&lt;div class="msg ok"&gt;&lt;b&gt;Doğru.&lt;/b&gt;' + e.why + '&lt;/div&gt;' : '') + '&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;';
}
const DEFAULT_GRID = [{ t: 'Kategoriler', c: 'Kategori' }, { t: 'Tarih', c: 'Yil' }];
function daxView(e, k) {
  const solved = P.ex[k]; const isQ = e.t === 'query';
  let h = '&lt;div class="ex-grid"&gt;&lt;div class="panel brief"&gt;&lt;div class="row" style="justify-content:space-between"&gt;&lt;span class="eyebrow"&gt;' + (isQ ? 'DAX sorgusu' : 'Ölçü yaz') + (solved ? ' · çözüldü' : '') + '&lt;/span&gt;' + (!isQ ? '&lt;span class="chip dx"&gt;' + esc(e.name) + '&lt;/span&gt;' : '') + '&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class="q"&gt;' + e.q + '&lt;/div&gt;';
  if (!isQ) { const g = e.grid || DEFAULT_GRID; h += '&lt;div class="note" style="margin:0"&gt;&lt;b&gt;Nasıl kontrol edilir?&lt;/b&gt;Ölçün ' + (e.noTotal ? '' : 'toplamda ve ') + g.map(x =&gt; '&lt;code&gt;' + colKey(x) + '&lt;/code&gt;').join(' ile ') + ' kırılımlarında hesaplanır ve doğru çözümle karşılaştırılır. Hazır ölçüler (ör. &lt;code&gt;[Net Satış]&lt;/code&gt;, &lt;code&gt;[Hedef]&lt;/code&gt;) kullanılabilir.&lt;/div&gt;'; }
  h += '&lt;div class="row" style="gap:8px"&gt;' + (e.hint ? '&lt;button class="btn sm" data-act="hint"&gt;İpucu&lt;/button&gt;' : '') + '&lt;button class="btn sm ghost" data-act="sol"&gt;Çözümü göster&lt;/button&gt;&lt;button class="btn sm ghost" data-act="ex-studio"&gt;Stüdyoda aç&lt;/button&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div id="hintBox"&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div id="solBox"&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;';
  h += '&lt;div class="work"&gt;&lt;div class="ed-shell"&gt;&lt;div class="ed-top"&gt;&lt;span&gt;' + (isQ ? 'EVALUATE sorgusu' : esc(e.name) + ' =') + '&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;Ctrl/⌘ + Enter: kontrol et&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div id="ed"&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class="ed-actions"&gt;&lt;button class="btn pri" data-act="check"&gt;Kontrol et&lt;/button&gt;&lt;button class="btn" data-act="run"&gt;Yalnızca çalıştır&lt;/button&gt;&lt;button class="btn ghost sm" data-act="reset-ed"&gt;Baştan başla&lt;/button&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div id="out"&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;';
  return h;
}
function mountLesson() {
  const l = LMAP[S.id]; if (!l) return;
  if (S.step === 0) { if (!P.seen[l.id]) { P.seen[l.id] = true; save(); } return; }
  const e = (l.ex || [])[S.step - 1]; if (!e || e.t === 'mc') return;
  const k = exKey(l, S.step - 1); const init = P.drafts[k] != null ? P.drafts[k] : (e.start || (e.t === 'query' ? 'EVALUATE\n' : ''));
  ED = makeEditor($('#ed'), init, () =&gt; checkEx(true), e.t === 'query');
  ED.onChange = v =&gt; { P.drafts[k] = v; save(); };
  if (P.ex[k] &amp;&amp; P.drafts[k] != null) checkEx(false, true);
}
function cmpVal(a, b) {
  const bl = v =&gt; v === null || v === undefined || (typeof v === 'number' &amp;&amp; isNaN(v));
  if (bl(a) &amp;&amp; bl(b)) return true; if (bl(a) || bl(b)) return false;
  if (typeof a === 'number' &amp;&amp; typeof b === 'number') return Math.abs(a - b) &lt;= 1e-6 * Math.max(1, Math.abs(b));
  if (a instanceof Date &amp;&amp; b instanceof Date) return a.getTime() === b.getTime();
  return String(a) === String(b);
}
function gridEval(ms, expr, grid, noTotal) {
  const out = [];
  if (!noTotal) out.push({ g: 'Toplam', rows: [{ k: 'Toplam', v: X.evalMeasure(ms, expr, []) }] });
  grid.forEach(col =&gt; { const r = X.query({ rows: [col], measures: ms, values: [expr], slicers: [], totals: false, keepBlank: true }); out.push({ g: colKey(col), rows: r.rows.map(x =&gt; ({ k: x.keys[0], v: x.vals[0] })) }); });
  return out;
}
function checkEx(grade, quiet) {
  const l = LMAP[S.id], i = S.step - 1, e = l.ex[i], k = exKey(l, i); const out = $('#out'); const src = ED.get();
  if (!src.trim() || (e.t === 'query' &amp;&amp; !/EVALUATE[\s\S]*\S/.test(src.replace(/^\s*EVALUATE\s*$/, '')))) { out.innerHTML = '&lt;div class="msg info"&gt;&lt;b&gt;Editör boş&lt;/b&gt;Önce bir ifade yaz.&lt;/div&gt;'; return; }
  try {
    if (e.t === 'query') {
      const mine = X.runQuery(measureSet(), src); let html = resultTable(mine.cols, mine.rows);
      if (!grade) { out.innerHTML = html; return; }
      const ref = X.runQuery(measureSet(), e.sol);
      const keyRow = r =&gt; r.map(v =&gt; typeof v === 'number' ? Math.round(v * 1e6) / 1e6 : v instanceof Date ? v.getTime() : v).join('␟');
      const a = mine.rows.map(keyRow).sort(), b = ref.rows.map(keyRow).sort();
      const ok = mine.cols.length === ref.cols.length &amp;&amp; a.length === b.length &amp;&amp; a.every((x, j) =&gt; x === b[j]);
      if (ok) { solved(l, i, k, quiet); out.innerHTML = '&lt;div class="msg ok"&gt;&lt;b&gt;Doğru!&lt;/b&gt;Sorgun beklenen ' + ref.rows.length + ' satırı döndürdü.&lt;/div&gt;' + html; }
      else out.innerHTML = '&lt;div class="msg err"&gt;&lt;b&gt;Henüz değil&lt;/b&gt;' + (mine.cols.length !== ref.cols.length ? 'Beklenen sütun sayısı ' + ref.cols.length + ', seninki ' + mine.cols.length + '.' : 'Beklenen ' + ref.rows.length + ' satır, seninki ' + mine.rows.length + ' satır' + (a.length === b.length ? ' ama değerler farklı.' : '.')) + '&lt;/div&gt;' + html;
      return;
    }
    const ms = measureSet(e.name); const grid = e.grid || DEFAULT_GRID;
    const expr = X.stripDef(src).expr; const err0 = X.check(src); if (err0) throw new X.DaxError(err0);
    const mine = gridEval(ms, expr, grid, e.noTotal);
    if (!grade) { out.innerHTML = gridHTML(mine, null, e.name); return; }
    const ref = gridEval(measureSet(e.name), e.sol, grid, e.noTotal);
    let bad = 0, total = 0;
    ref.forEach((G, gi) =&gt; G.rows.forEach((r, ri) =&gt; { total++; const m = (mine[gi].rows.find(x =&gt; X.keyOf(x.k) === X.keyOf(r.k)) || {}).v; if (!cmpVal(m, r.v)) bad++; }));
    if (!bad) { solved(l, i, k, quiet); out.innerHTML = '&lt;div class="msg ok"&gt;&lt;b&gt;Doğru!&lt;/b&gt;' + total + ' hücrenin tamamı beklenen sonuçla aynı.' + (e.why ? ' ' + e.why : '') + '&lt;/div&gt;' + gridHTML(mine, ref, e.name); }
    else out.innerHTML = '&lt;div class="msg err"&gt;&lt;b&gt;Henüz değil&lt;/b&gt;' + total + ' hücreden ' + bad + ' tanesi beklenenden farklı. Farklı hücreler işaretli; “beklenen” değerleri ipucu olarak kullan.&lt;/div&gt;' + gridHTML(mine, ref, e.name);
  } catch (er) {
    if (!er.dax) console.error(er);
    out.innerHTML = '&lt;div class="msg err"&gt;&lt;b&gt;DAX hatası&lt;/b&gt;&lt;span class="vend"&gt;' + esc(er.message) + '&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;';
  }
}
function solved(l, i, k, quiet) {
  if (!P.ex[k]) { P.ex[k] = true; addXP(l.ex[i].t === 'mc' ? 5 : 10); if (!quiet) toast('+' + (l.ex[i].t === 'mc' ? 5 : 10) + ' XP'); save(); }
  const b = document.querySelector('.steps button[data-step="' + (i + 1) + '"]'); if (b) b.classList.add('done');
  if (checkLessonDone(l)) { const p = document.querySelector('.pager'); if (p) p.lastElementChild.outerHTML = nextBtn(l); }
}
function gridHTML(mine, ref, name) {
  const f = fmtOf(name);
  return '&lt;div class="panel result"&gt;' + mine.map((G, gi) =&gt; {
    const R = ref &amp;&amp; ref[gi];
    let h = '&lt;div class="rset"&gt;&lt;div class="result-meta"&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;b&gt;' + esc(G.g) + '&lt;/b&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;' + G.rows.length + ' satır&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class="rtable"&gt;&lt;table&gt;&lt;thead&gt;&lt;tr&gt;&lt;th&gt;' + (G.g === 'Toplam' ? '' : esc(G.g)) + '&lt;/th&gt;&lt;th class="numc"&gt;Senin sonucun&lt;/th&gt;' + (R ? '&lt;th class="numc"&gt;Beklenen&lt;/th&gt;' : '') + '&lt;/tr&gt;&lt;/thead&gt;&lt;tbody&gt;';
    const rows = R ? R.rows : G.rows;
    rows.forEach(r =&gt; { const m = R ? (G.rows.find(x =&gt; X.keyOf(x.k) === X.keyOf(r.k)) || {}).v : r.v; const ok = !R || cmpVal(m, r.v); h += '&lt;tr class="' + (ok ? '' : 'bad') + '"&gt;&lt;td&gt;' + esc(V.fmt(r.k)) + '&lt;/td&gt;&lt;td class="numc"&gt;' + esc(fmtVal(m, f)) + '&lt;/td&gt;' + (R ? '&lt;td class="numc"&gt;' + esc(fmtVal(r.v, f)) + '&lt;/td&gt;' : '') + '&lt;/tr&gt;'; });
    return h + '&lt;/tbody&gt;&lt;/table&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;';
  }).join('') + '&lt;/div&gt;';
}
function fmtVal(v, f) { if (v === null || v === undefined) return '(Boş)'; if (typeof v === 'number') { if (f === 'yuzde') return V.fmt(v, 'yuzde'); if (f === 'para') return V.fmt(v, 'para'); return V.fmt(v); } return V.fmt(v); }
function resultTable(cols, rows, lim) {
  lim = lim || 200;
  let h = '&lt;div class="panel result"&gt;&lt;div class="result-meta"&gt;&lt;span&gt;' + rows.length + ' satır · ' + cols.length + ' sütun&lt;/span&gt;' + (rows.length &gt; lim ? '&lt;span&gt;İlk ' + lim + ' satır gösteriliyor&lt;/span&gt;' : '') + '&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class="rtable"&gt;&lt;table&gt;&lt;thead&gt;&lt;tr&gt;' + cols.map(c =&gt; '&lt;th&gt;' + esc(c) + '&lt;/th&gt;').join('') + '&lt;/tr&gt;&lt;/thead&gt;&lt;tbody&gt;';
  rows.slice(0, lim).forEach(r =&gt; { h += '&lt;tr&gt;' + r.map((v, j) =&gt; { const f = guessFmt(cols[j]); return '&lt;td class="' + (typeof v === 'number' ? 'numc' : '') + (v === null ? ' null' : '') + '"&gt;' + esc(v === null ? '(Boş)' : typeof v === 'number' ? fmtVal(v, f &amp;&amp; /%/.test(cols[j]) ? 'yuzde' : null) : V.fmt(v)) + '&lt;/td&gt;'; }).join('') + '&lt;/tr&gt;'; });
  if (!rows.length) h += '&lt;tr&gt;&lt;td class="null"&gt;Sonuç boş&lt;/td&gt;&lt;/tr&gt;';
  return h + '&lt;/tbody&gt;&lt;/table&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;';
}

/* ---------- Editör ---------- */
function makeEditor(host, init, onSubmit, isQ) {
  const keys = ['[', ']', '(', ')', '"', ',', '=', '&amp;', '{', '}', 'CALCULATE(', 'SUM(', 'DIVIDE(', 'FILTER(', 'ALL(', 'VAR ', 'RETURN '].concat(isQ ? ['EVALUATE', 'SUMMARIZECOLUMNS('] : []);
  host.innerHTML = '&lt;div class="ed"&gt;&lt;pre aria-hidden="true"&gt;&lt;code&gt;&lt;/code&gt;&lt;/pre&gt;&lt;textarea spellcheck="false" autocapitalize="off" autocomplete="off" autocorrect="off" aria-label="DAX editörü" id="daxEditor"&gt;&lt;/textarea&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class="ed-keys"&gt;' + keys.map(k =&gt; '&lt;button type="button" data-k="' + esc(k) + '"&gt;' + esc(k.trim()) + '&lt;/button&gt;').join('') + '&lt;/div&gt;';
  const ta = $('textarea', host), code = $('code', host), pre = $('pre', host);
  const api = { get: () =&gt; ta.value, set: v =&gt; { ta.value = v; paint(); api.onChange &amp;&amp; api.onChange(v); }, onChange: null, focus: () =&gt; ta.focus(), insert: t =&gt; insert(t) };
  function paint() { code.innerHTML = hl(ta.value) + '\n'; pre.scrollTop = ta.scrollTop; pre.scrollLeft = ta.scrollLeft; }
  ta.value = init; paint();
  ta.addEventListener('input', () =&gt; { paint(); api.onChange &amp;&amp; api.onChange(ta.value); });
  ta.addEventListener('scroll', () =&gt; { pre.scrollTop = ta.scrollTop; pre.scrollLeft = ta.scrollLeft; });
  ta.addEventListener('keydown', ev =&gt; {
    if ((ev.ctrlKey || ev.metaKey) &amp;&amp; ev.key === 'Enter') { ev.preventDefault(); onSubmit &amp;&amp; onSubmit(); }
    else if (ev.key === 'Tab' &amp;&amp; !ev.shiftKey) { ev.preventDefault(); insert('    '); }
    else if (ev.key === 'Enter') { const before = ta.value.slice(0, ta.selectionStart); const ln = before.split('\n').pop(); const ind = (ln.match(/^\s*/) || [''])[0] + (/[(,]\s*$/.test(ln) ? '    ' : ''); ev.preventDefault(); insert('\n' + ind); }
  });
  function insert(t) { const s = ta.selectionStart, e = ta.selectionEnd; ta.setRangeText(t, s, e, 'end'); ta.dispatchEvent(new Event('input')); }
  host.querySelectorAll('[data-k]').forEach(b =&gt; b.addEventListener('click', () =&gt; { insert(b.dataset.k); ta.focus(); }));
  return api;
}

/* ---------- Veri Modeli ---------- */
let modelSel = 'Satislar';
const ER_POS = { Satislar: [300, 150], Urunler: [560, 40], Kategoriler: [800, 150], Musteriler: [30, 40], Tarih: [30, 300], Hedefler: [560, 330] };
function viewModel() {
  let h = '&lt;div class="wrap"&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span class="eyebrow"&gt;Veri modeli&lt;/span&gt;&lt;h1&gt;Anadolu Market yıldız şeması&lt;/h1&gt;&lt;p class="muted" style="max-width:70ch"&gt;Ortada iki olgu tablosu (&lt;b&gt;Satislar&lt;/b&gt;, &lt;b&gt;Hedefler&lt;/b&gt;), çevresinde boyutlar. Oklar filtrenin aktığı yönü gösterir: bir (1) taraftan çok (*) tarafa. Bir tabloya dokunarak sütunlarını ve ilk satırlarını gör.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/div&gt;';
  h += '&lt;div class="panel er" id="er"&gt;' + erSVG() + '&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div id="tdetail"&gt;&lt;/div&gt;';
  h += '&lt;div class="panel" style="padding:18px 20px"&gt;&lt;h3 style="margin-bottom:8px"&gt;Bu modeldeki ilişkiler&lt;/h3&gt;&lt;div class="coltable"&gt;&lt;table&gt;&lt;thead&gt;&lt;tr&gt;&lt;th&gt;Bir (1)&lt;/th&gt;&lt;th&gt;Çok (*)&lt;/th&gt;&lt;th&gt;Kardinalite&lt;/th&gt;&lt;th&gt;Filtre yönü&lt;/th&gt;&lt;/tr&gt;&lt;/thead&gt;&lt;tbody&gt;' + MODEL.rels.map(r =&gt; '&lt;tr&gt;&lt;td&gt;' + r.to + '[' + r.toCol + ']&lt;/td&gt;&lt;td&gt;' + r.from + '[' + r.fromCol + ']&lt;/td&gt;&lt;td&gt;Bire-çok&lt;/td&gt;&lt;td&gt;Tek&lt;/td&gt;&lt;/tr&gt;').join('') + '&lt;/tbody&gt;&lt;/table&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;';
  return h + '&lt;/div&gt;';
}
function erSVG() {
  const Wd = 200, rowH = 16, head = 26; let lines = '', boxes = '';
  const boxH = t =&gt; head + MODEL.tables[t].cols.length * rowH + 8;
  const anchor = (t, c) =&gt; { const [x, y] = ER_POS[t]; const i = MODEL.tables[t].cols.indexOf(c); return [x, y + head + i * rowH + rowH / 2 + 2]; };
  MODEL.rels.forEach(r =&gt; { const [x1, y1] = anchor(r.to, r.toCol), [x2, y2] = anchor(r.from, r.fromCol); const a = x1 &lt; x2 ? [x1 + Wd, y1] : [x1, y1], b = x1 &lt; x2 ? [x2, y2] : [x2 + Wd, y2]; const mx = (a[0] + b[0]) / 2; lines += '&lt;path d="M' + a[0] + ',' + a[1] + ' C' + mx + ',' + a[1] + ' ' + mx + ',' + b[1] + ' ' + b[0] + ',' + b[1] + '"/&gt;&lt;text class="card1" x="' + (a[0] + (x1 &lt; x2 ? 6 : -10)) + '" y="' + (a[1] - 4) + '"&gt;1&lt;/text&gt;&lt;text class="card1" x="' + (b[0] + (x1 &lt; x2 ? -10 : 4)) + '" y="' + (b[1] - 4) + '"&gt;*&lt;/text&gt;'; });
  X.TNAMES.forEach(t =&gt; { const [x, y] = ER_POS[t]; const tb = MODEL.tables[t]; const fact = tb.kind === 'fact'; boxes += '&lt;g class="tbox' + (t === modelSel ? ' sel' : '') + (fact ? ' fact' : '') + '" data-tbl="' + t + '" tabindex="0" role="button" aria-label="' + t + ' tablosu"&gt;&lt;rect x="' + x + '" y="' + y + '" width="' + Wd + '" height="' + boxH(t) + '" rx="8"/&gt;&lt;path class="hd" d="M' + x + ',' + (y + head) + 'V' + (y + 8) + 'Q' + x + ',' + y + ' ' + (x + 8) + ',' + y + 'H' + (x + Wd - 8) + 'Q' + (x + Wd) + ',' + y + ' ' + (x + Wd) + ',' + (y + 8) + 'V' + (y + head) + 'Z"/&gt;&lt;text class="t" x="' + (x + 10) + '" y="' + (y + 17) + '"&gt;' + t + '&lt;/text&gt;&lt;text class="k" x="' + (x + Wd - 10) + '" y="' + (y + 17) + '" text-anchor="end"&gt;' + (fact ? 'OLGU' : 'BOYUT') + '&lt;/text&gt;' + tb.cols.map((c, i) =&gt; { const isPk = MODEL.rels.some(r =&gt; r.to === t &amp;&amp; r.toCol === c), isFk = MODEL.rels.some(r =&gt; r.from === t &amp;&amp; r.fromCol === c); return '&lt;text x="' + (x + 10) + '" y="' + (y + head + i * rowH + 13) + '"&gt;' + c + '&lt;/text&gt;' + (isPk ? '&lt;text class="k" x="' + (x + Wd - 10) + '" y="' + (y + head + i * rowH + 13) + '" text-anchor="end"&gt;1&lt;/text&gt;' : isFk ? '&lt;text class="fk" x="' + (x + Wd - 10) + '" y="' + (y + head + i * rowH + 13) + '" text-anchor="end"&gt;*&lt;/text&gt;' : ''); }).join('') + '&lt;/g&gt;'; });
  return '&lt;svg viewBox="0 0 1010 520" role="img" aria-label="Yıldız şema diyagramı"&gt;' + lines + boxes + '&lt;/svg&gt;';
}
function mountModel() { showTable(modelSel); }
function showTable(t) {
  modelSel = t; const er = $('#er'); if (er) er.innerHTML = erSVG();
  const tb = MODEL.tables[t]; const rows = tb.rows.slice(0, 8);
  let h = '&lt;div class="panel" style="padding:18px 20px;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:12px"&gt;&lt;div class="row" style="justify-content:space-between"&gt;&lt;h2&gt;' + t + '&lt;/h2&gt;&lt;span class="chip"&gt;' + tb.rows.length.toLocaleString('tr-TR') + ' satır · ' + (tb.kind === 'fact' ? 'olgu' : 'boyut') + '&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;p class="muted" style="margin:0"&gt;' + esc(MODEL.notes[t]) + '&lt;/p&gt;';
  h += '&lt;div class="coltable"&gt;&lt;table&gt;&lt;thead&gt;&lt;tr&gt;&lt;th&gt;Sütun&lt;/th&gt;&lt;th&gt;Tür&lt;/th&gt;&lt;th&gt;Açıklama&lt;/th&gt;&lt;/tr&gt;&lt;/thead&gt;&lt;tbody&gt;' + tb.cols.map(c =&gt; { const v = tb.rows.find(r =&gt; r[c] != null)[c]; const ty = v instanceof Date ? 'Tarih' : typeof v === 'number' ? (Number.isInteger(v) ? 'Tam sayı' : 'Ondalık') : 'Metin'; return '&lt;tr&gt;&lt;td&gt;' + c + '&lt;/td&gt;&lt;td&gt;' + ty + '&lt;/td&gt;&lt;td&gt;' + esc(MODEL.colDesc[t + '[' + c + ']'] || '') + '&lt;/td&gt;&lt;/tr&gt;'; }).join('') + '&lt;/tbody&gt;&lt;/table&gt;&lt;/div&gt;';
  h += '&lt;span class="eyebrow"&gt;İlk ' + rows.length + ' satır&lt;/span&gt;' + resultTable(tb.cols, rows.map(r =&gt; tb.cols.map(c =&gt; r[c] === undefined ? null : r[c])));
  h += '&lt;div class="row"&gt;&lt;button class="btn sm" data-try="EVALUATE TOPN(20, ' + t + ')"&gt;Stüdyoda sorgula&lt;/button&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;';
  $('#tdetail').innerHTML = h;
}

/* ---------- DAX Stüdyosu ---------- */
const ST = Object.assign({ mode: 'm', code: { m: 'Net Satış Payı % =\nDIVIDE(\n    [Net Satış],\n    CALCULATE([Net Satış], ALL(Kategoriler))\n)', q: 'EVALUATE\nSUMMARIZECOLUMNS(\n    Kategoriler[Kategori],\n    "Net Satış", [Net Satış],\n    "Hedef", [Hedef],\n    "Gerçekleşme", [Hedef Gerçekleşme %]\n)\nORDER BY [Net Satış] DESC' }, axis: 'Kategoriler[Kategori]', axis2: '', chart: 'column', yil: [2024], kanal: [], side: 'fields' }, P.studio || {});
function saveStudio() { P.studio = { mode: ST.mode, code: ST.code, axis: ST.axis, axis2: ST.axis2, chart: ST.chart, yil: ST.yil, kanal: ST.kanal, side: ST.side }; save(); }
const STUDIO_EX = [
  ['Kategori payı', 'm', 'Net Satış Payı % =\nDIVIDE([Net Satış], CALCULATE([Net Satış], ALL(Kategoriler)))', 'Kategoriler[Kategori]'],
  ['Yıllık büyüme', 'm', 'YoY % =\nVAR BuYil = [Net Satış]\nVAR GecenYil = CALCULATE([Net Satış], SAMEPERIODLASTYEAR(Tarih[Tarih]))\nRETURN DIVIDE(BuYil - GecenYil, GecenYil)', 'Kategoriler[Kategori]'],
  ['Aylık YTD', 'm', 'Satış YTD = TOTALYTD([Net Satış], Tarih[Tarih])', 'Tarih[YilAy]', 'line'],
  ['Bağlam geçişi farkı', 'm', '// SUM satır bağlamını filtreye çevirmez:\nSUMX(Musteriler, SUM(Satislar[Tutar]))', 'Satislar[Kanal]'],
  ['Ürün sırası', 'm', 'Ürün Sırası = IF(HASONEVALUE(Urunler[Urun]), RANKX(ALL(Urunler[Urun]), [Net Satış]))', 'Urunler[Urun]', 'table'],
  ['Hareketli ortalama', 'm', '3 Ay Ort. =\nVAR Donem = DATESINPERIOD(Tarih[Tarih], MAX(Tarih[Tarih]), -3, MONTH)\nRETURN DIVIDE(CALCULATE([Net Satış], Donem), 3)', 'Tarih[YilAy]', 'line'],
  ['İlk 5 ürün (sorgu)', 'q', 'EVALUATE\nTOPN(5, SUMMARIZECOLUMNS(Urunler[Urun], Urunler[Kategori], "Net Satış", [Net Satış], "Marj", [Kâr Marjı %]), [Net Satış])\nORDER BY [Net Satış] DESC'],
  ['Segment × kanal (sorgu)', 'q', 'EVALUATE\nSUMMARIZECOLUMNS(\n    Musteriler[Segment],\n    Satislar[Kanal],\n    TREATAS({2024}, Tarih[Yil]),\n    "Net Satış", [Net Satış]\n)'.replace('    TREATAS({2024}, Tarih[Yil]),\n', '    FILTER(ALL(Tarih[Yil]), Tarih[Yil] = 2024),\n')],
  ['Ölçü tanımlı sorgu', 'q', 'DEFINE\n    MEASURE Satislar[Ortalama İndirimli Satır] =\n        AVERAGEX(FILTER(Satislar, Satislar[Indirim] &gt; 0), Satislar[Tutar])\nEVALUATE\nADDCOLUMNS(VALUES(Satislar[Kanal]), "Ort. İndirimli", [Ortalama İndirimli Satır])']
];
let SED = null;
function viewStudio() {
  const axisOpts = (sel, none) =&gt; (none ? '&lt;option value=""&gt;Yok&lt;/option&gt;' : '') + X.TNAMES.map(t =&gt; '&lt;optgroup label="' + t + '"&gt;' + MODEL.tables[t].cols.map(c =&gt; { const k = t + '[' + c + ']'; return '&lt;option value="' + k + '"' + (k === sel ? ' selected' : '') + '&gt;' + c + '&lt;/option&gt;'; }).join('') + '&lt;/optgroup&gt;').join('');
  let h = '&lt;div class="wrap" style="gap:18px"&gt;&lt;div class="sec-head"&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span class="eyebrow"&gt;Oyun alanı&lt;/span&gt;&lt;h1&gt;DAX Stüdyosu&lt;/h1&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class="dtoggle" role="tablist"&gt;&lt;button data-smode="m" aria-pressed="' + (ST.mode === 'm') + '"&gt;Ölçü&lt;/button&gt;&lt;button data-smode="q" aria-pressed="' + (ST.mode === 'q') + '"&gt;Sorgu (EVALUATE)&lt;/button&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;';
  h += '&lt;p class="muted" style="margin:-8px 0 0;max-width:80ch"&gt;' + (ST.mode === 'm' ? 'Bir ölçü yaz, satır eksenini seç; Power BI gibi her satır için ayrı filtre bağlamında hesaplanır. Toplam satırı ayrı hesaplanır. &lt;code&gt;Ad = ifade&lt;/code&gt; yazıp kaydedersen ölçü “Ölçülerim” listesine girer ve başka ölçülerde kullanılabilir.' : 'DAX sorgu görünümü gibi: &lt;code&gt;EVALUATE&lt;/code&gt; ile bir tablo döndür, &lt;code&gt;DEFINE MEASURE&lt;/code&gt; ile geçici ölçü tanımla, &lt;code&gt;ORDER BY&lt;/code&gt; ile sırala.') + '&lt;/p&gt;';
  h += '&lt;div class="studio"&gt;&lt;aside class="panel fields"&gt;&lt;div class="dtoggle sm"&gt;&lt;button data-side="fields" aria-pressed="' + (ST.side === 'fields') + '"&gt;Alanlar&lt;/button&gt;&lt;button data-side="measures" aria-pressed="' + (ST.side === 'measures') + '"&gt;Ölçüler&lt;/button&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div id="sideList"&gt;' + sideList() + '&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/aside&gt;';
  h += '&lt;div class="work"&gt;&lt;div class="ed-shell"&gt;&lt;div class="ed-top"&gt;&lt;label&gt;Örnek yükle &lt;select id="stEx"&gt;&lt;option value=""&gt;Seç…&lt;/option&gt;' + STUDIO_EX.map((x, i) =&gt; x[1] === ST.mode ? '&lt;option value="' + i + '"&gt;' + esc(x[0]) + '&lt;/option&gt;' : '').join('') + '&lt;/select&gt;&lt;/label&gt;&lt;span&gt;Ctrl/⌘ + Enter: çalıştır&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div id="sed"&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;';
  h += '&lt;div class="ed-actions"&gt;&lt;button class="btn pri" data-act="st-run"&gt;Çalıştır&lt;/button&gt;' + (ST.mode === 'm' ? '&lt;button class="btn" data-act="st-save"&gt;Ölçü olarak kaydet&lt;/button&gt;' : '') + '&lt;button class="btn ghost sm" data-act="st-clear"&gt;Temizle&lt;/button&gt;&lt;/div&gt;';
  if (ST.mode === 'm') {
    h += '&lt;div class="panel vset"&gt;&lt;label&gt;Satır ekseni&lt;select id="stAxis"&gt;' + axisOpts(ST.axis) + '&lt;/select&gt;&lt;/label&gt;&lt;label&gt;Sütun ekseni&lt;select id="stAxis2"&gt;' + axisOpts(ST.axis2, true) + '&lt;/select&gt;&lt;/label&gt;&lt;label&gt;Görsel&lt;select id="stChart"&gt;' + [['table', 'Matris'], ['column', 'Sütun'], ['bar', 'Çubuk'], ['line', 'Çizgi'], ['donut', 'Halka'], ['card', 'Kart']].map(([v, t]) =&gt; '&lt;option value="' + v + '"' + (v === ST.chart ? ' selected' : '') + '&gt;' + t + '&lt;/option&gt;').join('') + '&lt;/select&gt;&lt;/label&gt;';
    h += '&lt;div class="slrow"&gt;&lt;span class="eyebrow"&gt;Dilimleyici: Yıl&lt;/span&gt;' + [2022, 2023, 2024].map(y =&gt; '&lt;button class="chip" data-styil="' + y + '" aria-pressed="' + ST.yil.includes(y) + '"&gt;' + y + '&lt;/button&gt;').join('') + '&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class="slrow"&gt;&lt;span class="eyebrow"&gt;Kanal&lt;/span&gt;' + ['Web', 'Mobil', 'Mağaza'].map(k =&gt; '&lt;button class="chip" data-stkanal="' + k + '" aria-pressed="' + ST.kanal.includes(k) + '"&gt;' + k + '&lt;/button&gt;').join('') + '&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;';
  }
  h += '&lt;div id="stOut"&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;';
  return h;
}
function sideList() {
  if (ST.side === 'fields') return X.TNAMES.map(t =&gt; '&lt;details' + (t === 'Satislar' ? ' open' : '') + '&gt;&lt;summary&gt;' + t + '&lt;span&gt;' + MODEL.tables[t].rows.length + '&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/summary&gt;&lt;ul&gt;' + MODEL.tables[t].cols.map(c =&gt; '&lt;li&gt;&lt;button data-ins="' + esc(t + '[' + c + ']') + '"&gt;' + c + '&lt;/button&gt;&lt;/li&gt;').join('') + '&lt;/ul&gt;&lt;/details&gt;').join('') + '&lt;p class="muted" style="font-size:.78rem;margin:8px 4px 0"&gt;Bir alana dokununca editöre eklenir.&lt;/p&gt;';
  const groups = {}; LIB.forEach(m =&gt; (groups[m.group] = groups[m.group] || []).push(m));
  let h = '';
  if ((P.mine || []).length) h += '&lt;details open&gt;&lt;summary&gt;Ölçülerim&lt;span&gt;' + P.mine.length + '&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/summary&gt;&lt;ul&gt;' + P.mine.map((m, i) =&gt; '&lt;li class="mm"&gt;&lt;button data-ins="[' + esc(m.name) + ']" title="' + esc(m.expr) + '"&gt;' + esc(m.name) + '&lt;/button&gt;&lt;button class="x" data-act="mine-edit" data-i="' + i + '" aria-label="Düzenle"&gt;✎&lt;/button&gt;&lt;button class="x" data-act="mine-del" data-i="' + i + '" aria-label="Sil"&gt;✕&lt;/button&gt;&lt;/li&gt;').join('') + '&lt;/ul&gt;&lt;/details&gt;';
  Object.keys(groups).forEach(g =&gt; { h += '&lt;details' + (g === 'Temel' ? ' open' : '') + '&gt;&lt;summary&gt;' + g + '&lt;span&gt;' + groups[g].length + '&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/summary&gt;&lt;ul&gt;' + groups[g].map(m =&gt; '&lt;li&gt;&lt;button data-ins="[' + esc(m.name) + ']" title="' + esc(m.name + ' = ' + m.expr) + '"&gt;' + esc(m.name) + '&lt;/button&gt;&lt;/li&gt;').join('') + '&lt;/ul&gt;&lt;/details&gt;'; });
  return h + '&lt;p class="muted" style="font-size:.78rem;margin:8px 4px 0"&gt;Hazır ölçüler bu uygulamanın dashboardlarında kullanılır. Üzerine gelince formülü görünür; dokununca editöre eklenir.&lt;/p&gt;';
}
function mountStudio() {
  SED = makeEditor($('#sed'), ST.code[ST.mode] || '', studioRun, ST.mode === 'q');
  SED.onChange = v =&gt; { ST.code[ST.mode] = v; saveStudio(); };
  studioRun();
}
function upsertMine(name, expr, quiet) {
  P.mine = P.mine || []; const i = P.mine.findIndex(m =&gt; m.name.toLowerCase() === name.toLowerCase());
  if (LIBMAP[name]) { if (!quiet) toast('“' + name + '” hazır bir ölçünün adı; başka bir ad seç.'); return false; }
  if (i &gt;= 0) P.mine[i].expr = expr; else P.mine.push({ name, expr }); save(); if (!quiet) toast('Kaydedildi: [' + name + ']'); return true;
}
function studioRun() {
  const out = $('#stOut'); if (!out) return; const src = SED ? SED.get() : ST.code[ST.mode];
  if (!src.trim()) { out.innerHTML = '&lt;div class="panel empty"&gt;Bir ifade yaz ya da yukarıdan bir örnek yükle.&lt;/div&gt;'; return; }
  const t0 = performance.now();
  try {
    if (ST.mode === 'q') { const r = X.runQuery(measureSet(), src); out.innerHTML = '&lt;div class="timing"&gt;' + Math.round(performance.now() - t0) + ' ms&lt;/div&gt;' + resultTable(r.cols, r.rows); return; }
    const d = X.stripDef(src); const err0 = X.check(src); if (err0) throw new X.DaxError(err0);
    const name = d.name || 'Ölçü'; const ms = measureSet(d.name); const f = fmtOf(name);
    const sl = []; if (ST.yil.length) sl.push({ t: 'Tarih', c: 'Yil', values: ST.yil }); if (ST.kanal.length) sl.push({ t: 'Satislar', c: 'Kanal', values: ST.kanal });
    if (ST.chart === 'card') { const v = X.evalMeasure(ms, d.expr, sl); out.innerHTML = '&lt;div class="timing"&gt;' + Math.round(performance.now() - t0) + ' ms&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class="panel vbox" style="max-width:320px"&gt;' + V.card(v, name, f) + '&lt;/div&gt;'; return; }
    const a1 = parseCol(ST.axis), a2 = ST.axis2 ? parseCol(ST.axis2) : null;
    if (a2) {
      const r = X.query({ rows: [a1, a2], measures: ms, values: [d.expr], slicers: sl, totals: true });
      const k1 = [], k2 = []; r.rows.forEach(x =&gt; { if (!k1.some(v =&gt; X.keyOf(v) === X.keyOf(x.keys[0]))) k1.push(x.keys[0]); if (!k2.some(v =&gt; X.keyOf(v) === X.keyOf(x.keys[1]))) k2.push(x.keys[1]); });
      k2.sort((a, b) =&gt; X.sortCompare(a2, a, b));
      const cell = (a, b) =&gt; { const x = r.rows.find(z =&gt; X.keyOf(z.keys[0]) === X.keyOf(a) &amp;&amp; X.keyOf(z.keys[1]) === X.keyOf(b)); return x ? x.vals[0] : null; };
      const rowTot = k1.map(a =&gt; X.evalMeasure(ms, d.expr, sl.filter(s =&gt; !(s.t === a1.t &amp;&amp; s.c === a1.c)).concat([{ t: a1.t, c: a1.c, values: [a] }]), { shadow: sl }));
      const colTot = k2.map(b =&gt; X.evalMeasure(ms, d.expr, sl.filter(s =&gt; !(s.t === a2.t &amp;&amp; s.c === a2.c)).concat([{ t: a2.t, c: a2.c, values: [b] }]), { shadow: sl }));
      const rows = k1.map((a, i) =&gt; [V.fmt(a)].concat(k2.map(b =&gt; cell(a, b))).concat([rowTot[i]]));
      out.innerHTML = '&lt;div class="timing"&gt;' + r.rows.length + ' hücre · ' + Math.round(performance.now() - t0) + ' ms&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class="panel vbox"&gt;&lt;div class="vtitle"&gt;' + esc(name) + ' · ' + esc(a1.c) + ' × ' + esc(a2.c) + '&lt;/div&gt;' + V.table([a1.c].concat(k2.map(b =&gt; V.fmt(b))).concat(['Toplam']), rows, { fmts: [null].concat(k2.map(() =&gt; f)).concat([f]), cf: Object.fromEntries(k2.map((b, i) =&gt; [i + 1, 'heat'])), total: ['Toplam'].concat(colTot).concat(r.total) }) + '&lt;/div&gt;';
      bindMarks(out); return;
    }
    const r = X.query({ rows: [a1], measures: ms, values: [d.expr], slicers: sl, totals: true });
    const cats = r.rows.map(x =&gt; V.fmt(x.keys[0])), vals = r.rows.map(x =&gt; x.vals[0]);
    const numeric = vals.every(v =&gt; v === null || typeof v === 'number');
    let vis = '';
    if (!numeric || ST.chart === 'table' || !r.rows.length) vis = V.table([a1.c, name], r.rows.map(x =&gt; [V.fmt(x.keys[0]), x.vals[0]]), { fmts: [null, f], cf: numeric ? { 1: 'bar' } : null, total: ['Toplam', r.total[0]] });
    else {
      const data = { cats, series: [{ name, vals }], fmt: f };
      vis = ST.chart === 'bar' ? V.bar(data) : ST.chart === 'line' ? V.line(data, { area: true }) : ST.chart === 'donut' ? V.donut(data) : V.column(data, { labels: true });
      vis += '&lt;div class="vtot"&gt;&lt;span&gt;Toplam satırı&lt;/span&gt;&lt;b&gt;' + esc(fmtVal(r.total[0], f)) + '&lt;/b&gt;&lt;/div&gt;';
      vis += '&lt;details class="vdet"&gt;&lt;summary&gt;Matris olarak gör&lt;/summary&gt;' + V.table([a1.c, name], r.rows.map(x =&gt; [V.fmt(x.keys[0]), x.vals[0]]), { fmts: [null, f], total: ['Toplam', r.total[0]] }) + '&lt;/details&gt;';
    }
    out.innerHTML = '&lt;div class="timing"&gt;' + r.rows.length + ' satır · ' + Math.round(performance.now() - t0) + ' ms' + (!numeric &amp;&amp; ST.chart !== 'table' ? ' · metin sonuçlar matris olarak gösterildi' : '') + '&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class="panel vbox"&gt;&lt;div class="vtitle"&gt;' + esc(name) + ' · ' + esc(a1.c) + (sl.length ? ' &lt;span class="muted"&gt;(' + sl.map(s =&gt; s.values.join(', ')).join(' · ') + ')&lt;/span&gt;' : '') + '&lt;/div&gt;' + vis + '&lt;/div&gt;';
    bindMarks(out);
  } catch (er) {
    if (!er.dax) console.error(er);
    out.innerHTML = '&lt;div class="msg err"&gt;&lt;b&gt;DAX hatası&lt;/b&gt;&lt;span class="vend"&gt;' + esc(er.message) + '&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;';
  }
}
let tipsBound = false;
function bindMarks(el) { if (!tipsBound) { V.bindTips(document.body); tipsBound = true; } }

/* ---------- Formüller ---------- */
let fq = { cat: 'Tümü', q: '' };
function viewFormulas() {
  const cats = ['Tümü'].concat([...new Set(window.PBI_CATALOG.map(x =&gt; x[1]))]);
  return '&lt;div class="wrap"&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span class="eyebrow"&gt;Başvuru&lt;/span&gt;&lt;h1&gt;DAX formülleri&lt;/h1&gt;&lt;p class="muted" style="max-width:68ch"&gt;' + window.PBI_CATALOG.length + ' sık kullanılan fonksiyon. Her örnek bu modelde çalışır: “Stüdyoda aç” ile sonucu matris ve grafikte gör.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class="row"&gt;&lt;input class="input" id="fQ" placeholder="Fonksiyon ara: CALCULATE, tarih, sıra…" value="' + esc(fq.q) + '" style="max-width:340px" aria-label="Fonksiyon ara"&gt;&lt;div class="filters"&gt;' + cats.map(c =&gt; '&lt;button data-fcat="' + esc(c) + '" aria-pressed="' + (fq.cat === c) + '"&gt;' + esc(c) + '&lt;/button&gt;').join('') + '&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class="fgrid" id="fList"&gt;' + fList() + '&lt;/div&gt;' + libSection() + '&lt;/div&gt;';
}
function fList() {
  const q = fq.q.trim().toLocaleLowerCase('tr');
  const items = window.PBI_CATALOG.filter(x =&gt; (fq.cat === 'Tümü' || x[1] === fq.cat) &amp;&amp; (!q || (x.join(' ')).toLocaleLowerCase('tr').includes(q)));
  if (!items.length) return '&lt;div class="panel empty"&gt;Aramana uyan fonksiyon yok.&lt;/div&gt;';
  return items.map(([n, c, syn, d, ex]) =&gt; '&lt;article class="panel fcard"&gt;&lt;div class="row" style="justify-content:space-between"&gt;&lt;h3&gt;' + esc(n) + '&lt;/h3&gt;&lt;span class="chip"&gt;' + esc(c) + '&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;code class="syn"&gt;' + esc(syn) + '&lt;/code&gt;&lt;p&gt;' + esc(d) + '&lt;/p&gt;&lt;pre class="dax ex"&gt;' + esc(ex) + '&lt;/pre&gt;&lt;/article&gt;').join('');
}
function libSection() {
  return '&lt;section class="lvl-block"&gt;&lt;div class="sec-head"&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span class="eyebrow"&gt;Hazır ölçüler&lt;/span&gt;&lt;h2&gt;Bu modelin ölçü kütüphanesi&lt;/h2&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;p class="muted" style="margin:0;max-width:70ch"&gt;Dashboardlar ve alıştırmalar bu ölçüleri kullanır. İyi bir model, ölçüleri birbirinin üzerine kurar: temel ölçüler bir kez tanımlanır, diğerleri onları çağırır.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;div class="panel"&gt;&lt;div class="coltable"&gt;&lt;table&gt;&lt;thead&gt;&lt;tr&gt;&lt;th&gt;Ölçü&lt;/th&gt;&lt;th&gt;Formül&lt;/th&gt;&lt;th&gt;Açıklama&lt;/th&gt;&lt;/tr&gt;&lt;/thead&gt;&lt;tbody&gt;' + LIB.map(m =&gt; '&lt;tr&gt;&lt;td&gt;[' + esc(m.name) + ']&lt;/td&gt;&lt;td&gt;&lt;code class="mcode"&gt;' + hl(m.expr) + '&lt;/code&gt;&lt;/td&gt;&lt;td&gt;' + esc(m.desc) + '&lt;/td&gt;&lt;/tr&gt;').join('') + '&lt;/tbody&gt;&lt;/table&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/section&gt;';
}

/* ---------- Görseller ---------- */
let visSel = 'bar';
function viewVisuals() {
  const cats = [...new Set(window.PBI_VISUALS.map(v =&gt; v.cat))];
  let h = '&lt;div class="wrap"&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span class="eyebrow"&gt;Görselleştirme&lt;/span&gt;&lt;h1&gt;Görsel galerisi&lt;/h1&gt;&lt;p class="muted" style="max-width:70ch"&gt;Her görsel türü için ne zaman kullanılacağı, nelerden kaçınılacağı ve alan kutularına ne konacağı. Canlı örnekler Anadolu Market verisinden, DAX motoruyla hesaplanıyor.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class="vis-layout"&gt;&lt;nav class="vgal" aria-label="Görsel türleri"&gt;';
  cats.forEach(c =&gt; { h += '&lt;span class="eyebrow"&gt;' + esc(c) + '&lt;/span&gt;&lt;div class="vgal-row"&gt;' + window.PBI_VISUALS.filter(v =&gt; v.cat === c).map(v =&gt; '&lt;button data-vis="' + v.id + '" aria-pressed="' + (v.id === visSel) + '"&gt;' + V.thumb(v.id) + '&lt;span&gt;' + esc(v.name) + '&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/button&gt;').join('') + '&lt;/div&gt;'; });
  h += '&lt;/nav&gt;&lt;div id="visDetail"&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;';
  return h;
}
function mountVisuals() { showVisual(visSel); }
function showVisual(id) {
  visSel = id; document.querySelectorAll('[data-vis]').forEach(b =&gt; b.setAttribute('aria-pressed', b.dataset.vis === id));
  const v = window.PBI_VISUALS.find(x =&gt; x.id === id); const live = GALLERY[id];
  let h = '&lt;div class="panel vdetail"&gt;&lt;div class="row" style="justify-content:space-between"&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span class="eyebrow"&gt;' + esc(v.cat) + '&lt;/span&gt;&lt;h2&gt;' + esc(v.name) + '&lt;/h2&gt;&lt;/div&gt;' + V.thumb(id) + '&lt;/div&gt;';
  h += '&lt;div class="vwhen"&gt;&lt;div class="tip"&gt;&lt;b&gt;Ne zaman?&lt;/b&gt;' + esc(v.when) + '&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class="warn"&gt;&lt;b&gt;Kaçın&lt;/b&gt;' + esc(v.avoid) + '&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class="note"&gt;&lt;b&gt;Alan kutuları&lt;/b&gt;' + esc(v.fields) + '&lt;/div&gt;';
  if (live) { let r; try { r = live(); } catch (e) { console.error(e); r = { html: '&lt;div class="msg err"&gt;' + esc(e.message) + '&lt;/div&gt;', dax: [] }; } h += '&lt;div class="vbox panel"&gt;&lt;div class="vtitle"&gt;' + esc(r.title || 'Canlı örnek') + '&lt;/div&gt;' + r.html + '&lt;/div&gt;' + daxBlock(r.dax); }
  else h += '&lt;div class="msg info"&gt;&lt;b&gt;Canlı örnek yok&lt;/b&gt;Bu görsel Power BI\'ın kendi motoruna ya da harita hizmetine dayanıyor. Kavram ve kullanım için yukarıdaki notlar yeterli; Power BI Desktop\'ta Görselleştirmeler bölmesinden ekleyebilirsin.&lt;/div&gt;';
  $('#visDetail').innerHTML = h + '&lt;/div&gt;'; enhance($('#visDetail')); bindMarks();
}
function daxBlock(names) {
  if (!names || !names.length) return '';
  const uniq = []; const add = n =&gt; { if (uniq.includes(n)) return; const m = LIBMAP[n]; if (!m) return; (m.expr.match(/\[([^\]]+)\]/g) || []).forEach(x =&gt; { const nn = x.slice(1, -1); if (LIBMAP[nn] &amp;&amp; !/\w\[/.test(x)) add(nn); }); uniq.push(n); };
  names.forEach(add);
  return '&lt;details class="daxshow"&gt;&lt;summary&gt;Bu görselin DAX ölçüleri (' + uniq.length + ')&lt;/summary&gt;&lt;pre class="dax" data-notry&gt;' + esc(uniq.map(n =&gt; n + ' = ' + LIBMAP[n].expr).join('\n\n')) + '&lt;/pre&gt;&lt;button class="btn sm" data-try="' + esc(uniq.map(n =&gt; n + ' = ' + LIBMAP[n].expr).join('\n')) + '"&gt;Son ölçüyü Stüdyoda aç&lt;/button&gt;&lt;/details&gt;';
}
/* sorgu yardımcıları */
const C = s =&gt; parseCol(s);
function Q(rowCols, values, slicers, opts) { return X.query(Object.assign({ rows: rowCols.map(C), measures: measureSet(), values, slicers: slicers || [], totals: true }, opts || {})); }
function series(r, names, fmts) { return names.map((n, i) =&gt; ({ name: n, vals: r.rows.map(x =&gt; x.vals[i]), fmt: fmts &amp;&amp; fmts[i] })); }
const Y24 = [{ t: 'Tarih', c: 'Yil', values: [2024] }];
const GALLERY = {
  card: () =&gt; { const v = X.evalMeasure(measureSet(), '[Net Satış]', Y24); return { title: 'Net satış, 2024', html: '&lt;div style="max-width:300px"&gt;' + V.card(v, 'Net Satış · 2024', 'para') + '&lt;/div&gt;', dax: ['Net Satış'] }; },
  kpi: () =&gt; { const ms = measureSet(); return { title: 'Net satış ve hedef, 2024', html: '&lt;div style="max-width:300px"&gt;' + V.kpi(X.evalMeasure(ms, '[Net Satış]', Y24), X.evalMeasure(ms, '[Hedef]', Y24), 'Net Satış · 2024', 'para') + '&lt;/div&gt;', dax: ['Net Satış', 'Hedef'] }; },
  gauge: () =&gt; { const ms = measureSet(); const v = X.evalMeasure(ms, '[Net Satış]', Y24), t = X.evalMeasure(ms, '[Hedef]', Y24); return { title: 'Hedefe göre net satış, 2024', html: '&lt;div style="max-width:340px;margin:auto"&gt;' + V.gauge(v, t, t * 1.3, 'para', 'Gösterge') + '&lt;/div&gt;', dax: ['Net Satış', 'Hedef'] }; },
  bar: () =&gt; { const r = Q(['Kategoriler[Kategori]'], ['Net Satış'], Y24); r.rows.sort((a, b) =&gt; b.vals[0] - a.vals[0]); return { title: 'Kategoriye göre net satış, 2024', html: V.bar({ cats: r.rows.map(x =&gt; x.keys[0]), series: [{ name: 'Net Satış', vals: r.rows.map(x =&gt; x.vals[0]) }], fmt: 'para' }), dax: ['Net Satış'] }; },
  column: () =&gt; { const r = Q(['Satislar[Kanal]'], ['Net Satış'], Y24); return { title: 'Kanala göre net satış, 2024', html: V.column({ cats: r.rows.map(x =&gt; x.keys[0]), series: [{ name: 'Net Satış', vals: r.rows.map(x =&gt; x.vals[0]) }], fmt: 'para' }, { labels: true }), dax: ['Net Satış'] }; },
  stacked: () =&gt; { const r = Q(['Tarih[Yil]', 'Satislar[Kanal]'], ['Net Satış']); const yrs = [2022, 2023, 2024], ks = ['Web', 'Mobil', 'Mağaza']; return { title: 'Yıl ve kanala göre net satış', html: V.column({ cats: yrs.map(String), series: ks.map(k =&gt; ({ name: k, vals: yrs.map(y =&gt; (r.rows.find(x =&gt; x.keys[0] === y &amp;&amp; x.keys[1] === k) || { vals: [null] }).vals[0]) })), fmt: 'para' }, { stacked: true }), dax: ['Net Satış'] }; },
  pct: () =&gt; { const r = Q(['Musteriler[Segment]', 'Satislar[Kanal]'], ['Net Satış'], Y24); const sg = ['Bireysel', 'Kurumsal', 'Premium'], ks = ['Web', 'Mobil', 'Mağaza']; return { title: 'Segmentlerin kanal karışımı, 2024', html: V.bar({ cats: sg, series: ks.map(k =&gt; ({ name: k, vals: sg.map(s =&gt; (r.rows.find(x =&gt; x.keys[0] === s &amp;&amp; x.keys[1] === k) || { vals: [null] }).vals[0]) })), fmt: 'para' }, { pct: true }), dax: ['Net Satış'] }; },
  donut: () =&gt; { const r = Q(['Satislar[Kanal]'], ['Net Satış'], Y24); return { title: 'Kanal payı, 2024', html: V.donut({ cats: r.rows.map(x =&gt; x.keys[0]), series: [{ name: 'Net Satış', vals: r.rows.map(x =&gt; x.vals[0]) }], fmt: 'para' }), dax: ['Net Satış'] }; },
  treemap: () =&gt; { const r = Q(['Urunler[Urun]'], ['Net Satış'], Y24); r.rows.sort((a, b) =&gt; b.vals[0] - a.vals[0]); const top = r.rows.slice(0, 14); return { title: 'En çok satan 14 ürün, 2024', html: V.treemap({ cats: top.map(x =&gt; x.keys[0]), series: [{ name: 'Net Satış', vals: top.map(x =&gt; x.vals[0]) }], fmt: 'para' }), dax: ['Net Satış'] }; },
  line: () =&gt; { const r = Q(['Tarih[Ay]'], ['Net Satış', 'Satış GY'], Y24); return { title: 'Aylık net satış: 2024 ve geçen yıl', html: V.line({ cats: r.rows.map(x =&gt; x.keys[0]), series: [{ name: '2024', vals: r.rows.map(x =&gt; x.vals[0]) }, { name: '2023 (GY)', vals: r.rows.map(x =&gt; x.vals[1]), dash: true }], fmt: 'para' }), dax: ['Net Satış', 'Satış GY'] }; },
  area: () =&gt; { const r = Q(['Tarih[Ay]'], ['Satış YTD'], Y24); return { title: 'Yılbaşından bugüne net satış, 2024', html: V.line({ cats: r.rows.map(x =&gt; x.keys[0]), series: [{ name: 'Satış YTD', vals: r.rows.map(x =&gt; x.vals[0]) }], fmt: 'para' }, { area: true }), dax: ['Satış YTD'] }; },
  combo: () =&gt; { const r = Q(['Tarih[Ay]'], ['Net Satış', 'Hedef'], Y24); return { title: 'Aylık net satış ve hedef, 2024', html: V.column({ cats: r.rows.map(x =&gt; x.keys[0]), series: [{ name: 'Net Satış', vals: r.rows.map(x =&gt; x.vals[0]) }, { name: 'Hedef', vals: r.rows.map(x =&gt; x.vals[1]), type: 'line', color: 'var(--s2)' }], fmt: 'para' }), dax: ['Net Satış', 'Hedef'] }; },
  waterfall: () =&gt; { const r = Q(['Kategoriler[Kategori]'], ['Net Satış', 'Satış GY'], Y24); const gy = X.evalMeasure(measureSet(), '[Satış GY]', Y24); return { title: '2023 → 2024 net satış değişimi, kategoriye göre', html: V.waterfall({ cats: r.rows.map(x =&gt; x.keys[0]), series: [{ name: 'Değişim', vals: r.rows.map(x =&gt; (x.vals[0] || 0) - (x.vals[1] || 0)) }], fmt: 'para' }, { start: gy, startLabel: '2023' }), dax: ['Net Satış', 'Satış GY'] }; },
  funnel: () =&gt; { const ms = measureSet(); const tot = X.evalMeasure(ms, 'COUNTROWS(Satislar)', Y24); const notC = X.evalMeasure(ms, 'CALCULATE(COUNTROWS(Satislar), Satislar[Durum] &lt;&gt; "İptal")', Y24); const notR = X.evalMeasure(ms, 'CALCULATE(COUNTROWS(Satislar), Satislar[Durum] IN {"Teslim Edildi", "Kargoda"})', Y24); const del = X.evalMeasure(ms, 'CALCULATE(COUNTROWS(Satislar), Satislar[Durum] = "Teslim Edildi")', Y24); return { title: 'Sipariş satırlarının yolculuğu, 2024', html: V.funnel({ cats: ['Tüm satırlar', 'İptal edilmeyen', 'İade edilmeyen', 'Teslim edilen'], series: [{ name: 'Satır', vals: [tot, notC, notR, del] }], fmt: 'adet' }), dax: [] }; },
  scatter: () =&gt; { const r = Q(['Urunler[Urun]', 'Urunler[Kategori]'], ['Net Satış', 'Kâr Marjı %'], Y24); return { title: 'Ürünler: net satış ve kâr marjı, 2024', html: V.scatter(r.rows.map(x =&gt; ({ label: x.keys[0], g: x.keys[1], x: x.vals[0], y: x.vals[1] })), { xfmt: 'para', yfmt: 'yuzde', xlabel: 'Net Satış', ylabel: 'Kâr Marjı' }), dax: ['Net Satış', 'Kâr Marjı %'] }; },
  matrix: () =&gt; { const r = Q(['Kategoriler[Kategori]', 'Tarih[Yil]'], ['Net Satış']); const ks = [...new Set(r.rows.map(x =&gt; x.keys[0]))]; const ys = [2022, 2023, 2024]; return { title: 'Kategori × yıl, ısı haritası biçimli', html: V.table(['Kategori'].concat(ys.map(String)), ks.map(k =&gt; [k].concat(ys.map(y =&gt; (r.rows.find(x =&gt; x.keys[0] === k &amp;&amp; x.keys[1] === y) || { vals: [null] }).vals[0]))), { fmts: [null, 'para', 'para', 'para'], cf: { 1: 'heat', 2: 'heat', 3: 'heat' } }), dax: ['Net Satış'] }; },
  table: () =&gt; { const r = Q(['Urunler[Urun]'], ['Net Satış', 'Satılan Adet', 'Kâr Marjı %'], Y24); r.rows.sort((a, b) =&gt; b.vals[0] - a.vals[0]); return { title: 'İlk 10 ürün, 2024', html: V.table(['Ürün', 'Net Satış', 'Adet', 'Marj'], r.rows.slice(0, 10).map(x =&gt; [x.keys[0]].concat(x.vals)), { fmts: [null, 'para', 'adet', 'yuzde'], cf: { 1: 'bar' } }), dax: ['Net Satış', 'Satılan Adet', 'Kâr Marjı %'] }; },
  slicer: () =&gt; ({ title: 'Dilimleyici (Dashboardlar sayfasında çalışır)', html: V.slicer('demo', 'Yıl', [2022, 2023, 2024], [2024]) + V.slicer('demo2', 'Kanal', ['Web', 'Mobil', 'Mağaza'], []), dax: [] })
};

/* ---------- Dashboardlar ---------- */
const DB = { id: 'ozet', yil: [2024], kanal: [], segment: [], cross: null };
const DASHES = [
  { id: 'ozet', name: 'Yönetici Özeti', desc: 'İşler yolunda mı? Hedef, geçen yıl ve kanal kırılımıyla net satış.' },
  { id: 'urun', name: 'Ürün Performansı', desc: 'Hangi kategori ve ürün büyüyor, hangisi kâr bırakıyor?' },
  { id: 'musteri', name: 'Müşteri Analizi', desc: 'Kim alıyor, nereden ve hangi kanaldan?' },
  { id: 'hedef', name: 'Hedef ve Gerçekleşen', desc: 'Bütçeye göre durum: ay ve kategori bazında fark.' }
];
function dbSlicers() { const s = []; if (DB.yil.length) s.push({ t: 'Tarih', c: 'Yil', values: DB.yil }); if (DB.kanal.length) s.push({ t: 'Satislar', c: 'Kanal', values: DB.kanal }); if (DB.segment.length) s.push({ t: 'Musteriler', c: 'Segment', values: DB.segment }); return s; }
function viewDash() {
  if (S.id &amp;&amp; DASHES.some(d =&gt; d.id === S.id)) DB.id = S.id;
  const d = DASHES.find(x =&gt; x.id === DB.id);
  let h = '&lt;div class="wrap" style="gap:16px"&gt;&lt;div class="sec-head"&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span class="eyebrow"&gt;Örnek raporlar&lt;/span&gt;&lt;h1&gt;Dashboardlar&lt;/h1&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class="dtabs2" role="tablist"&gt;' + DASHES.map(x =&gt; '&lt;button data-dash="' + x.id + '" aria-pressed="' + (x.id === DB.id) + '"&gt;' + esc(x.name) + '&lt;/button&gt;').join('') + '&lt;/div&gt;';
  h += '&lt;div class="dash-frame"&gt;&lt;div class="dash-head"&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;h2&gt;' + esc(d.name) + '&lt;/h2&gt;&lt;p class="muted"&gt;' + esc(d.desc) + '&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class="dash-sl"&gt;' + V.slicer('yil', 'Yıl', [2022, 2023, 2024], DB.yil) + V.slicer('kanal', 'Kanal', ['Web', 'Mobil', 'Mağaza'], DB.kanal) + V.slicer('segment', 'Segment', ['Bireysel', 'Kurumsal', 'Premium'], DB.segment) + '&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div id="crossBar"&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class="dgrid2" id="dgrid"&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;';
  h += '&lt;p class="muted" style="font-size:.85rem;margin:0"&gt;Dilimleyicilerde birden çok seçim yapabilirsin; seçimi kaldırmak için tekrar dokun. Çubuk, dilim ve satırlara tıklamak diğer görselleri &lt;b&gt;çapraz filtreler&lt;/b&gt;. Her görselin altındaki “DAX” bölümü, arkadaki ölçüleri gösterir.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/div&gt;';
  return h;
}
function mountDash() { paintDash(); }
function crossSpec() { return DB.cross ? [{ t: DB.cross.t, c: DB.cross.c, values: [DB.cross.v] }] : []; }
function tile(span, title, body, dax, cross, sub) { return '&lt;section class="dtile s' + span + '"' + (cross ? ' data-cross="' + esc(cross) + '"' : '') + '&gt;&lt;div class="dt-head"&gt;&lt;h3&gt;' + esc(title) + '&lt;/h3&gt;' + (sub ? '&lt;span class="muted"&gt;' + esc(sub) + '&lt;/span&gt;' : '') + '&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class="dt-body"&gt;' + body + '&lt;/div&gt;' + daxBlock(dax) + '&lt;/section&gt;'; }
function paintDash() {
  const g = $('#dgrid'); if (!g) return;
  const base = dbSlicers(); const cs = crossSpec(); const all = base.concat(cs);
  const ms = measureSet(); const M = (e, f) =&gt; X.evalMeasure(ms, e, f || all, { shadow: base });
  const exceptSelf = (t, c) =&gt; base.concat(cs.filter(x =&gt; !(x.t === t &amp;&amp; x.c === c)));
  const selIdx = (cats, t, c) =&gt; DB.cross &amp;&amp; DB.cross.t === t &amp;&amp; DB.cross.c === c ? cats.findIndex(k =&gt; X.keyOf(k) === X.keyOf(DB.cross.v)) : null;
  const yearLabel = DB.yil.length === 1 ? String(DB.yil[0]) : DB.yil.length ? DB.yil.join(', ') : 'Tüm yıllar';
  const timeAxis = DB.yil.length === 1 ? 'Tarih[Ay]' : 'Tarih[YilAy]';
  $('#crossBar').innerHTML = DB.cross ? '&lt;div class="crossbar"&gt;&lt;span&gt;Çapraz filtre: &lt;b&gt;' + esc(DB.cross.c) + ' = ' + esc(V.fmt(DB.cross.v)) + '&lt;/b&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;button class="btn sm ghost" data-act="cross-clear"&gt;Temizle ✕&lt;/button&gt;&lt;/div&gt;' : '';
  let h = '';
  try {
    if (DB.id === 'ozet') {
      const ns = M('[Net Satış]'), hd = M('[Hedef]'), gy = M('[Satış GY]');
      h += tile(3, 'Net Satış', V.kpi(ns, hd, yearLabel, 'para', 'hedefe göre'), ['Net Satış', 'Hedef']);
      h += tile(3, 'Geçen yıla göre', V.kpi(ns, gy, 'Satış GY: ' + V.fmt(gy, 'para', true), 'para', 'geçen yıla göre'), ['YoY %']);
      h += tile(3, 'Brüt Kâr', V.card(M('[Brüt Kâr]'), 'Kâr marjı ' + V.fmt(M('[Kâr Marjı %]'), 'yuzde'), 'para'), ['Brüt Kâr', 'Kâr Marjı %']);
      h += tile(3, 'Siparişler', V.card(M('[Sipariş Sayısı]'), 'Ortalama sepet ' + V.fmt(M('[Ortalama Sepet]'), 'para'), 'adet'), ['Sipariş Sayısı', 'Ortalama Sepet']);
      const rl = Q([timeAxis], ['Net Satış', 'Satış GY'], all, { shadow: base });
      h += tile(8, 'Aylık net satış ve geçen yıl', V.line({ cats: rl.rows.map(x =&gt; x.keys[0]), series: [{ name: 'Net Satış', vals: rl.rows.map(x =&gt; x.vals[0]) }, { name: 'Geçen yıl', vals: rl.rows.map(x =&gt; x.vals[1]), dash: true, color: 'var(--axis)' }], fmt: 'para' }), ['Net Satış', 'Satış GY'], null, yearLabel);
      const rk = Q(['Satislar[Kanal]'], ['Net Satış'], exceptSelf('Satislar', 'Kanal'));
      h += tile(4, 'Kanal payı', V.donut({ cats: rk.rows.map(x =&gt; x.keys[0]), series: [{ name: 'Net Satış', vals: rk.rows.map(x =&gt; x.vals[0]) }], fmt: 'para' }, { sel: selIdx(rk.rows.map(x =&gt; x.keys[0]), 'Satislar', 'Kanal') }), ['Net Satış'], 'Satislar[Kanal]|' + JSON.stringify(rk.rows.map(x =&gt; x.keys[0])));
      const rc = Q(['Kategoriler[Kategori]'], ['Net Satış'], exceptSelf('Kategoriler', 'Kategori')); rc.rows.sort((a, b) =&gt; b.vals[0] - a.vals[0]);
      h += tile(6, 'Kategoriye göre net satış', V.bar({ cats: rc.rows.map(x =&gt; x.keys[0]), series: [{ name: 'Net Satış', vals: rc.rows.map(x =&gt; x.vals[0]) }], fmt: 'para' }, { sel: selIdx(rc.rows.map(x =&gt; x.keys[0]), 'Kategoriler', 'Kategori') }), ['Net Satış'], 'Kategoriler[Kategori]|' + JSON.stringify(rc.rows.map(x =&gt; x.keys[0])));
      const rp = Q(['Urunler[Urun]'], ['Net Satış', 'Kâr Marjı %'], exceptSelf('Urunler', 'Urun')); rp.rows.sort((a, b) =&gt; b.vals[0] - a.vals[0]); const top = rp.rows.slice(0, 6);
      h += tile(6, 'En çok satan ürünler', V.table(['Ürün', 'Net Satış', 'Marj'], top.map(x =&gt; [x.keys[0]].concat(x.vals)), { fmts: [null, 'para', 'yuzde'], cf: { 1: 'bar' }, sel: selIdx(top.map(x =&gt; x.keys[0]), 'Urunler', 'Urun') }), ['Net Satış', 'Kâr Marjı %'], 'Urunler[Urun]|' + JSON.stringify(top.map(x =&gt; x.keys[0])));
    }
    if (DB.id === 'urun') {
      const rm = Q(['Kategoriler[Kategori]', 'Tarih[Yil]'], ['Net Satış'], all.filter(s =&gt; !(s.t === 'Tarih' &amp;&amp; s.c === 'Yil')), { shadow: base });
      const ks = [...new Set(rm.rows.map(x =&gt; x.keys[0]))], ys = [2022, 2023, 2024];
      h += tile(6, 'Kategori × yıl', V.table(['Kategori'].concat(ys.map(String)), ks.map(k =&gt; [k].concat(ys.map(y =&gt; (rm.rows.find(x =&gt; x.keys[0] === k &amp;&amp; x.keys[1] === y) || { vals: [null] }).vals[0]))), { fmts: [null, 'para', 'para', 'para'], cf: { 1: 'heat', 2: 'heat', 3: 'heat' }, sel: selIdx(ks, 'Kategoriler', 'Kategori') }), ['Net Satış'], 'Kategoriler[Kategori]|' + JSON.stringify(ks), 'Yıl dilimleyicisi bu görseli etkilemez');
      const rw = Q(['Kategoriler[Kategori]'], ['Net Satış', 'Satış GY'], exceptSelf('Kategoriler', 'Kategori'));
      const gyT = X.evalMeasure(ms, '[Satış GY]', exceptSelf('Kategoriler', 'Kategori'), { shadow: base });
      h += tile(6, 'Geçen yıldan bu yıla değişim', DB.yil.length === 1 &amp;&amp; DB.yil[0] &gt; 2022 ? V.waterfall({ cats: rw.rows.map(x =&gt; x.keys[0]), series: [{ name: 'Değişim', vals: rw.rows.map(x =&gt; (x.vals[0] || 0) - (x.vals[1] || 0)) }], fmt: 'para' }, { start: gyT, startLabel: String(DB.yil[0] - 1) }) : '&lt;div class="empty"&gt;Şelale için Yıl dilimleyicisinde 2023 ya da 2024\'ü tek başına seç.&lt;/div&gt;', ['Net Satış', 'Satış GY'], null, 'Kategori katkıları');
      const rs = Q(['Urunler[Urun]', 'Urunler[Kategori]'], ['Net Satış', 'Kâr Marjı %'], exceptSelf('Urunler', 'Urun'));
      h += tile(7, 'Ürünler: satış ve kâr marjı', V.scatter(rs.rows.map(x =&gt; ({ label: x.keys[0], g: x.keys[1], x: x.vals[0], y: x.vals[1] })), { xfmt: 'para', yfmt: 'yuzde', xlabel: 'Net Satış', ylabel: 'Kâr Marjı' }), ['Net Satış', 'Kâr Marjı %']);
      const rt = Q(['Urunler[Urun]'], ['Net Satış'], exceptSelf('Urunler', 'Urun')); rt.rows.sort((a, b) =&gt; b.vals[0] - a.vals[0]); const tt = rt.rows.slice(0, 12);
      h += tile(5, 'Ürün payları', V.treemap({ cats: tt.map(x =&gt; x.keys[0]), series: [{ name: 'Net Satış', vals: tt.map(x =&gt; x.vals[0]) }], fmt: 'para' }, { sel: selIdx(tt.map(x =&gt; x.keys[0]), 'Urunler', 'Urun') }), ['Net Satış'], 'Urunler[Urun]|' + JSON.stringify(tt.map(x =&gt; x.keys[0])), 'İlk 12 ürün');
      const rd = Q(['Urunler[Urun]'], ['Net Satış', 'Satılan Adet', 'Kâr Marjı %', 'YoY %', 'İade Oranı %'], all); rd.rows.sort((a, b) =&gt; b.vals[0] - a.vals[0]);
      h += tile(12, 'Ürün ayrıntısı', V.table(['Ürün', 'Net Satış', 'Adet', 'Marj', 'YoY', 'İade'], rd.rows.slice(0, 15).map(x =&gt; [x.keys[0]].concat(x.vals)), { fmts: [null, 'para', 'adet', 'yuzde', 'yuzde', 'yuzde'], cf: { 1: 'bar', 4: 'icon' }, cfTarget: 0, total: ['Toplam', M('[Net Satış]'), M('[Satılan Adet]'), M('[Kâr Marjı %]'), M('[YoY %]'), M('[İade Oranı %]')] }), ['Net Satış', 'Satılan Adet', 'Kâr Marjı %', 'YoY %', 'İade Oranı %'], null, 'İlk 15 ürün');
    }
    if (DB.id === 'musteri') {
      h += tile(3, 'Aktif müşteri', V.card(M('[Müşteri Sayısı]'), yearLabel, 'adet'), ['Müşteri Sayısı']);
      h += tile(3, 'Müşteri başı satış', V.card(M('[Müşteri Başı Satış]'), 'Net satış / müşteri', 'para'), ['Müşteri Başı Satış']);
      h += tile(3, 'Yeni müşteri', V.card(M('[Yeni Müşteri]'), 'İlk siparişi bu dönemde', 'adet'), ['Yeni Müşteri']);
      h += tile(3, 'Müşteri başı sipariş', V.card(DIV(M('[Sipariş Sayısı]'), M('[Müşteri Sayısı]')), 'Sipariş / müşteri', null), ['Sipariş Sayısı', 'Müşteri Sayısı']);
      const rsg = Q(['Musteriler[Segment]', 'Satislar[Kanal]'], ['Net Satış'], all); const sg = ['Bireysel', 'Kurumsal', 'Premium'].filter(s =&gt; rsg.rows.some(x =&gt; x.keys[0] === s)), kn = ['Web', 'Mobil', 'Mağaza'];
      h += tile(6, 'Segmentlerin kanal karışımı', V.bar({ cats: sg, series: kn.map(k =&gt; ({ name: k, vals: sg.map(s =&gt; (rsg.rows.find(x =&gt; x.keys[0] === s &amp;&amp; x.keys[1] === k) || { vals: [null] }).vals[0]) })), fmt: 'para' }, { pct: true }), ['Net Satış'], null, '%100 yığılmış');
      const rci = Q(['Musteriler[Sehir]'], ['Net Satış'], exceptSelf('Musteriler', 'Sehir')); rci.rows = rci.rows.filter(x =&gt; x.keys[0]); rci.rows.sort((a, b) =&gt; b.vals[0] - a.vals[0]); const c8 = rci.rows.slice(0, 8);
      h += tile(6, 'Şehre göre net satış', V.bar({ cats: c8.map(x =&gt; x.keys[0]), series: [{ name: 'Net Satış', vals: c8.map(x =&gt; x.vals[0]) }], fmt: 'para' }, { sel: selIdx(c8.map(x =&gt; x.keys[0]), 'Musteriler', 'Sehir') }), ['Net Satış'], 'Musteriler[Sehir]|' + JSON.stringify(c8.map(x =&gt; x.keys[0])), 'İlk 8 şehir');
      const ra = Q(['Musteriler[YasGrubu]'], ['Net Satış', 'Müşteri Sayısı'], exceptSelf('Musteriler', 'YasGrubu')); ra.rows.sort((a, b) =&gt; String(a.keys[0]).localeCompare(String(b.keys[0])));
      h += tile(5, 'Yaş grubuna göre', V.column({ cats: ra.rows.map(x =&gt; x.keys[0]), series: [{ name: 'Net Satış', vals: ra.rows.map(x =&gt; x.vals[0]) }], fmt: 'para' }, { labels: true, sel: selIdx(ra.rows.map(x =&gt; x.keys[0]), 'Musteriler', 'YasGrubu') }), ['Net Satış'], 'Musteriler[YasGrubu]|' + JSON.stringify(ra.rows.map(x =&gt; x.keys[0])));
      const rtop = Q(['Musteriler[Musteri]', 'Musteriler[Segment]', 'Musteriler[Sehir]'], ['Net Satış', 'Sipariş Sayısı'], all); rtop.rows.sort((a, b) =&gt; b.vals[0] - a.vals[0]);
      h += tile(7, 'En değerli 8 müşteri', V.table(['Müşteri', 'Segment', 'Şehir', 'Net Satış', 'Sipariş'], rtop.rows.slice(0, 8).map(x =&gt; x.keys.map(k =&gt; k == null ? '—' : k).concat(x.vals)), { fmts: [null, null, null, 'para', 'adet'], cf: { 3: 'bar' } }), ['Net Satış', 'Sipariş Sayısı', 'Müşteri Sırası']);
    }
    if (DB.id === 'hedef') {
      const ns = M('[Net Satış]'), hd = M('[Hedef]');
      h += tile(4, 'Hedef gerçekleşme', V.gauge(ns, hd, Math.max(ns || 0, hd || 0) * 1.25, 'para', 'Gösterge'), ['Net Satış', 'Hedef'], null, yearLabel);
      h += tile(4, 'Fark', V.kpi(M('[Hedef Farkı]'), 0, 'Net satış − hedef', 'para', 'sıfıra göre').replace('kpi ', 'kpi ' + ((M('[Hedef Farkı]') || 0) &gt;= 0 ? '' : '')), ['Hedef Farkı']);
      const rmo = Q([timeAxis], ['Net Satış', 'Hedef'], all); const hit = rmo.rows.filter(x =&gt; (x.vals[0] || 0) &gt;= (x.vals[1] || 0) &amp;&amp; x.vals[1]).length;
      h += tile(4, 'Hedefi tutturan ay', V.card(hit, rmo.rows.length + ' aydan', null, '&lt;em class="pill ' + (hit / Math.max(1, rmo.rows.length) &gt;= 0.5 ? 'ok' : 'bad') + '"&gt;' + (hit / Math.max(1, rmo.rows.length) &gt;= 0.5 ? '▲' : '▼') + ' %' + Math.round(hit / Math.max(1, rmo.rows.length) * 100) + '&lt;/em&gt;'), ['Net Satış', 'Hedef']);
      h += tile(12, 'Aylık net satış ve hedef', V.column({ cats: rmo.rows.map(x =&gt; x.keys[0]), series: [{ name: 'Net Satış', vals: rmo.rows.map(x =&gt; x.vals[0]) }, { name: 'Hedef', vals: rmo.rows.map(x =&gt; x.vals[1]), type: 'line', color: 'var(--s2)' }], fmt: 'para' }), ['Net Satış', 'Hedef']);
      const rk = Q(['Kategoriler[Kategori]'], ['Net Satış', 'Hedef', 'Hedef Farkı', 'Hedef Gerçekleşme %'], exceptSelf('Kategoriler', 'Kategori')); rk.rows.sort((a, b) =&gt; (b.vals[2] || 0) - (a.vals[2] || 0));
      h += tile(5, 'Kategoriye göre hedef farkı', V.bar({ cats: rk.rows.map(x =&gt; x.keys[0]), series: [{ name: 'Hedef Farkı', vals: rk.rows.map(x =&gt; x.vals[2]) }], fmt: 'para' }, { colorBySign: true, sel: selIdx(rk.rows.map(x =&gt; x.keys[0]), 'Kategoriler', 'Kategori') }), ['Hedef Farkı'], 'Kategoriler[Kategori]|' + JSON.stringify(rk.rows.map(x =&gt; x.keys[0])), 'Yeşil: hedef aşıldı');
      h += tile(7, 'Kategori özeti', V.table(['Kategori', 'Net Satış', 'Hedef', 'Fark', 'Gerçekleşme'], rk.rows.map(x =&gt; [x.keys[0]].concat(x.vals)), { fmts: [null, 'para', 'para', 'para', 'yuzde'], cf: { 4: 'icon' }, cfTarget: 1, total: ['Toplam', ns, hd, M('[Hedef Farkı]'), M('[Hedef Gerçekleşme %]')], sel: selIdx(rk.rows.map(x =&gt; x.keys[0]), 'Kategoriler', 'Kategori') }), ['Net Satış', 'Hedef', 'Hedef Farkı', 'Hedef Gerçekleşme %'], 'Kategoriler[Kategori]|' + JSON.stringify(rk.rows.map(x =&gt; x.keys[0])));
    }
  } catch (e) { console.error(e); h = '&lt;div class="msg err"&gt;&lt;b&gt;Hesaplama hatası&lt;/b&gt;' + esc(e.message) + '&lt;/div&gt;'; }
  g.innerHTML = h; enhance(g); bindMarks();
}
const DIV = (a, b) =&gt; (b ? a / b : null);

/* ---------- Seviye testi ---------- */
let T = null;
function viewTest() {
  const Q2 = window.PBI_PLACEMENT;
  if (!T) T = { i: 0, score: { temel: 0, orta: 0, ileri: 0 }, ans: [] };
  if (T.i &gt;= Q2.length) {
    const lv = T.score.ileri &gt;= 3 &amp;&amp; T.score.orta &gt;= 3 ? 'ileri' : T.score.orta &gt;= 3 &amp;&amp; T.score.temel &gt;= 3 ? 'orta' : 'temel';
    P.placement = lv; save();
    const total = T.ans.filter(Boolean).length;
    const rec = lv === 'ileri' ? 'gelistirici' : lv === 'orta' ? 'analist' : 'hizli';
    return '&lt;div class="wrap"&gt;&lt;div class="panel hero-main"&gt;&lt;span class="eyebrow"&gt;Sonuç&lt;/span&gt;&lt;h1&gt;' + total + '/' + Q2.length + ' doğru · &lt;em&gt;' + lvName(lv) + '&lt;/em&gt;&lt;/h1&gt;&lt;p&gt;' + (lv === 'temel' ? 'Temelden başlamak en hızlı yol olacak. “Bir Günde Başla” patikası seni kısa sürede ilk dashboarduna götürür.' : lv === 'orta' ? 'Temeli biliyorsun. Bağlamlar, CALCULATE ve zaman zekâsıyla devam et.' : 'Güçlü bir temelin var. İleri DAX, performans ve güvenlik konularına odaklan.') + '&lt;/p&gt;&lt;div class="row"&gt;&lt;button class="btn pri" data-go="path" data-id="' + rec + '"&gt;Önerilen patika: ' + esc(PMAP[rec].name) + '&lt;/button&gt;&lt;button class="btn" data-act="test-again"&gt;Testi tekrar çöz&lt;/button&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;' +
      '&lt;div class="panel lessons"&gt;' + LEVELS.map(L =&gt; '&lt;div class="litem" style="cursor:default"&gt;&lt;span&gt;' + lvBadge(L.id) + '&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;b&gt;' + L.name + '&lt;/b&gt;&lt;small&gt;' + T.score[L.id] + '/' + Q2.filter(q =&gt; q.lv === L.id).length + ' doğru&lt;/small&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;').join('') + '&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;';
  }
  const q = Q2[T.i];
  return '&lt;div class="wrap"&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span class="eyebrow"&gt;Seviye testi · ' + (T.i + 1) + '/' + Q2.length + '&lt;/span&gt;&lt;h1&gt;Nereden başlamalısın?&lt;/h1&gt;&lt;p class="muted"&gt;12 soru, yaklaşık 5 dakika. Bilmiyorsan tahmin etme; “Bilmiyorum” de.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class="bar"&gt;&lt;i style="width:' + (T.i / Q2.length * 100) + '%"&gt;&lt;/i&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class="panel brief" style="max-width:760px"&gt;' + lvBadge(q.lv) + '&lt;div class="q"&gt;' + esc(q.q) + '&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class="mc"&gt;' + q.o.map((o, i) =&gt; '&lt;button data-tq="' + i + '"&gt;&lt;span class="key"&gt;' + String.fromCharCode(65 + i) + '&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;' + esc(o) + '&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/button&gt;').join('') + '&lt;button data-tq="-1"&gt;&lt;span class="key"&gt;?&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;Bilmiyorum&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/button&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;';
}

/* ---------- Sözlük ---------- */
let dq = '';
function viewDict() { return '&lt;div class="wrap"&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span class="eyebrow"&gt;Sözlük&lt;/span&gt;&lt;h1&gt;Power BI terimleri&lt;/h1&gt;&lt;p class="muted"&gt;' + window.PBI_GLOSSARY.length + ' terim. Fonksiyonlar için &lt;button class="btn ghost sm" style="padding:0" data-go="formulas"&gt;DAX Formülleri&lt;/button&gt; sayfasına bak.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;input class="input" id="dQ" placeholder="Terim ara" value="' + esc(dq) + '" style="max-width:340px" aria-label="Terim ara"&gt;&lt;div class="panel dict" id="dList"&gt;' + dList() + '&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;'; }
function dList() { const q = dq.trim().toLocaleLowerCase('tr'); const it = window.PBI_GLOSSARY.filter(g =&gt; !q || (g[0] + ' ' + g[1]).toLocaleLowerCase('tr').includes(q)); return it.length ? '&lt;dl&gt;' + it.map(g =&gt; '&lt;div&gt;&lt;dt&gt;' + esc(g[0]) + '&lt;/dt&gt;&lt;dd&gt;' + esc(g[1]) + '&lt;/dd&gt;&lt;/div&gt;').join('') + '&lt;/dl&gt;' : '&lt;div class="empty"&gt;Sonuç yok.&lt;/div&gt;'; }

/* ---------- Olaylar ---------- */
document.addEventListener('click', ev =&gt; {
  const t = ev.target.closest('button, [data-tbl], a'); if (!t) return;
  const d = t.dataset;
  if (d.go) { ev.preventDefault(); if (d.lv) currFilter.lv = d.lv; return go(d.go, d.id); }
  if (d.lesson) return go('lesson', d.lesson, { ctx: d.ctx || (S.view === 'path' ? S.id : S.view === 'lesson' ? S.ctx : null) });
  if (d.step !== undefined) { S.step = +d.step; render(); window.scrollTo(0, 0); return; }
  if (d.lvf) { currFilter.lv = d.lvf; document.querySelectorAll('[data-lvf]').forEach(b =&gt; b.setAttribute('aria-pressed', b.dataset.lvf === d.lvf)); $('#currList').innerHTML = currList(); return; }
  if (d.mc !== undefined) {
    const l = LMAP[S.id], i = S.step - 1, e = l.ex[i], k = exKey(l, i); const ch = +d.mc;
    document.querySelectorAll('#mc button').forEach(b =&gt; b.classList.remove('right', 'wrong'));
    if (ch === e.a) { t.classList.add('right'); $('#mcFb').innerHTML = '&lt;div class="msg ok"&gt;&lt;b&gt;Doğru.&lt;/b&gt;' + (e.why || '') + '&lt;/div&gt;'; solved(l, i, k); }
    else { t.classList.add('wrong'); $('#mcFb').innerHTML = '&lt;div class="msg err"&gt;&lt;b&gt;Tekrar dene.&lt;/b&gt;Bu seçenek doğru değil.&lt;/div&gt;'; }
    return;
  }
  if (d.tq !== undefined) { const q = window.PBI_PLACEMENT[T.i]; const ok = +d.tq === q.a; if (ok) T.score[q.lv]++; T.ans.push(ok); T.i++; render(); return; }
  if (d.tbl) return showTable(d.tbl);
  if (d.try) return openInStudio(d.try);
  if (d.smode) { ST.mode = d.smode; saveStudio(); render(); return; }
  if (d.side) { ST.side = d.side; saveStudio(); document.querySelectorAll('[data-side]').forEach(b =&gt; b.setAttribute('aria-pressed', b.dataset.side === d.side)); $('#sideList').innerHTML = sideList(); return; }
  if (d.ins) { if (SED) { SED.insert(d.ins); SED.focus(); } return; }
  if (d.styil) { const y = +d.styil; ST.yil = ST.yil.includes(y) ? ST.yil.filter(x =&gt; x !== y) : ST.yil.concat([y]); t.setAttribute('aria-pressed', ST.yil.includes(y)); saveStudio(); studioRun(); return; }
  if (d.stkanal) { const k = d.stkanal; ST.kanal = ST.kanal.includes(k) ? ST.kanal.filter(x =&gt; x !== k) : ST.kanal.concat([k]); t.setAttribute('aria-pressed', ST.kanal.includes(k)); saveStudio(); studioRun(); return; }
  if (d.fcat) { fq.cat = d.fcat; document.querySelectorAll('[data-fcat]').forEach(b =&gt; b.setAttribute('aria-pressed', b.dataset.fcat === d.fcat)); $('#fList').innerHTML = fList(); enhance($('#fList')); return; }
  if (d.vis) return showVisual(d.vis);
  if (d.dash) { DB.id = d.dash; DB.cross = null; go('dash', d.dash); return; }
  if (d.slicer) {
    const v = JSON.parse(d.v); const key = d.slicer; if (!DB[key]) return;
    DB[key] = DB[key].includes(v) ? DB[key].filter(x =&gt; x !== v) : DB[key].concat([v]);
    t.setAttribute('aria-pressed', DB[key].includes(v)); paintDash(); return;
  }
  const act = d.act;
  if (act === 'hint') { const e = LMAP[S.id].ex[S.step - 1]; $('#hintBox').innerHTML = '&lt;div class="tip" style="margin:0"&gt;&lt;b&gt;İpucu&lt;/b&gt;' + esc(e.hint) + '&lt;/div&gt;'; return; }
  if (act === 'sol') { const e = LMAP[S.id].ex[S.step - 1]; $('#solBox').innerHTML = '&lt;div class="codebox"&gt;&lt;pre class="dax" data-done="1"&gt;' + hl((e.name ? e.name + ' =\n' : '') + e.sol) + '&lt;/pre&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class="row" style="margin-top:6px"&gt;&lt;button class="btn sm" data-act="take-sol"&gt;Editöre al&lt;/button&gt;&lt;/div&gt;'; return; }
  if (act === 'take-sol') { const e = LMAP[S.id].ex[S.step - 1]; ED.set(e.sol); return; }
  if (act === 'ex-studio') { const e = LMAP[S.id].ex[S.step - 1]; const src = ED.get(); openInStudio(e.t === 'query' ? src : (X.stripDef(src).name ? src : e.name + ' = ' + src)); return; }
  if (act === 'check') return checkEx(true);
  if (act === 'run') return checkEx(false);
  if (act === 'reset-ed') { const e = LMAP[S.id].ex[S.step - 1]; ED.set(e.start || (e.t === 'query' ? 'EVALUATE\n' : '')); $('#out').innerHTML = ''; return; }
  if (act === 'read-done') { const l = LMAP[S.id]; if (!P.done[l.id]) { P.done[l.id] = true; addXP(5); save(); } render(); return; }
  if (act === 'st-run') return studioRun();
  if (act === 'st-clear') { SED.set(''); $('#stOut').innerHTML = ''; return; }
  if (act === 'st-save') { const src = SED.get(); const dd = X.stripDef(src); if (!dd.name) { toast('Kaydetmek için ölçüye ad ver: Ad = ifade'); return; } const er = X.check(src); if (er) { toast('Önce hatayı düzelt: ' + er); return; } if (upsertMine(dd.name, dd.expr.trim())) { ST.side = 'measures'; saveStudio(); document.querySelectorAll('[data-side]').forEach(b =&gt; b.setAttribute('aria-pressed', b.dataset.side === 'measures')); $('#sideList').innerHTML = sideList(); } return; }
  if (act === 'mine-del') { const i = +d.i; P.mine.splice(i, 1); save(); $('#sideList').innerHTML = sideList(); toast('Ölçü silindi'); return; }
  if (act === 'mine-edit') { const m = P.mine[+d.i]; SED.set(m.name + ' =\n' + m.expr); studioRun(); return; }
  if (act === 'cross-clear') { DB.cross = null; paintDash(); return; }
  if (act === 'test-again') { T = null; render(); return; }
  if (act === 'reset-ask') { $('#resetBox').innerHTML = '&lt;span class="muted" style="font-size:.85rem"&gt;Tüm ilerleme, XP ve kayıtlı ölçüler silinecek.&lt;/span&gt;&lt;button class="btn sm" data-act="reset-yes"&gt;Evet, sıfırla&lt;/button&gt;&lt;button class="btn sm ghost" data-act="reset-no"&gt;Vazgeç&lt;/button&gt;'; return; }
  if (act === 'reset-yes') { P = { done: {}, ex: {}, xp: 0, days: {}, placement: null, drafts: {}, last: null, seen: {}, mine: [], studio: null }; save(); toast('İlerleme sıfırlandı'); render(); return; }
  if (act === 'reset-no') { render(); return; }
});
// dashboard çapraz filtre
document.addEventListener('click', ev =&gt; {
  const mark = ev.target.closest('.dtile [data-i]'); if (!mark) return; const tileEl = mark.closest('.dtile'); const cr = tileEl &amp;&amp; tileEl.dataset.cross; if (!cr) return;
  const [col, json] = [cr.slice(0, cr.indexOf('|')), cr.slice(cr.indexOf('|') + 1)]; const cats = JSON.parse(json); const v = cats[+mark.dataset.i]; const c = parseCol(col);
  if (v === undefined) return;
  if (DB.cross &amp;&amp; DB.cross.t === c.t &amp;&amp; DB.cross.c === c.c &amp;&amp; X.keyOf(DB.cross.v) === X.keyOf(v)) DB.cross = null; else DB.cross = { t: c.t, c: c.c, v };
  paintDash();
});
document.addEventListener('keydown', ev =&gt; { if ((ev.key === 'Enter' || ev.key === ' ') &amp;&amp; ev.target.matches('.dtile [data-i], [data-tbl]')) { ev.preventDefault(); ev.target.dispatchEvent(new MouseEvent('click', { bubbles: true })); } });
document.addEventListener('input', ev =&gt; {
  if (ev.target.id === 'currQ') { currFilter.q = ev.target.value; $('#currList').innerHTML = currList(); }
  if (ev.target.id === 'fQ') { fq.q = ev.target.value; $('#fList').innerHTML = fList(); enhance($('#fList')); }
  if (ev.target.id === 'dQ') { dq = ev.target.value; $('#dList').innerHTML = dList(); }
});
document.addEventListener('change', ev =&gt; {
  const id = ev.target.id;
  if (id === 'stAxis') { ST.axis = ev.target.value; saveStudio(); studioRun(); }
  if (id === 'stAxis2') { ST.axis2 = ev.target.value; saveStudio(); studioRun(); }
  if (id === 'stChart') { ST.chart = ev.target.value; saveStudio(); studioRun(); }
  if (id === 'stEx' &amp;&amp; ev.target.value !== '') { const x = STUDIO_EX[+ev.target.value]; SED.set(x[2]); if (x[3]) { ST.axis = x[3]; const a = $('#stAxis'); if (a) a.value = x[3]; } if (x[4]) { ST.chart = x[4]; const c = $('#stChart'); if (c) c.value = x[4]; } ST.axis2 = ''; const a2 = $('#stAxis2'); if (a2) a2.value = ''; saveStudio(); studioRun(); ev.target.value = ''; }
});

/* ---------- Başlat ---------- */
(function boot() {
  const h = (location.hash || '').slice(1);
  const back = {}; Object.keys(HASHV).forEach(k =&gt; back[HASHV[k]] = k);
  if (h.startsWith('ders-') &amp;&amp; LMAP[h.slice(5)]) S = { view: 'lesson', id: h.slice(5), step: 0, ctx: null };
  else if (h.startsWith('patika-') &amp;&amp; PMAP[h.slice(7)]) S = { view: 'path', id: h.slice(7), step: 0, ctx: h.slice(7) };
  else if (h.startsWith('dashboard-') &amp;&amp; DASHES.some(d =&gt; d.id === h.slice(10))) { S = { view: 'dash', id: h.slice(10) }; DB.id = h.slice(10); }
  else if (back[h]) S = { view: back[h], id: null, step: 0 };
  render();
})();
})();
